- 2026-08-14 (later — the quiz Customize panel: questions are the content): **Quiz-born activities now get their own Customize experience** (teacher's same-day feedback on setup mode: "it doesn't ask you about changing the questions... we need a customize just for the Speed Quiz"). New recipe field `setupPanel: "quiz"` (validated; only quiz-show declares it; `SetupKnobs.panelFor` gates on the same stamp/version agreement as the knobs, any mismatch degrades to the generic dialog). For these games the generic AI words-interview is SKIPPED entirely — the scout-confirmed hazard was real: `/api/games/revise` can rewrite a quiz's choices with no guarantee `correctAnswer` still matches, and it desyncs the provenance stamp. Instead the dialog IS the question editor: (1) **topic box** ("Want new questions? Give a topic") + how-many → new `POST /api/games/quiz-questions` → `AIService.generateQuizQuestions` (Sonnet, wrong facts are the failure mode; class profile rides along for difficulty; mock mode returns canned questions so the flow tests keyless) through `engine/quiz-questions.js` `cleanQuizQuestions` (drop-don't-fail per question: 2-6 choices, correct must exactly match a choice, cap 20 — mirrors the storyboard quiz brick's rules and accepts both `question`/`text` spellings); (2) **the question list is always visible and editable in place** — per-question card with text input, per-choice rows with a ✓/○ mark-the-answer toggle (editing the marked choice keeps its ✓; blank rows can never be silently marked), per-choice and per-question ✕, "+ choice" (max 6) and "+ Add a question" (max 20), the wrong-facts review gate built into the dialog itself; (3) timer + speed bonus knobs below; (4) "Make my copy" validates client-side (`SetupKnobs.validateQuizList`, problems named per question) then recompiles via the shared `compileWorkingConfig` (extracted from the generic dialog) and lands highlighted in the library as before. The count knob is gone for panel games (the visible list IS the count). Fill-in-the-blank format deliberately deferred (needs graded typed answers in the engine; owner picked multiple-choice-first). Verified: live Sonnet endpoint returns fact-checked-correct fraction questions; full browser flow (topic "planets of the solar system" → 5 generated questions → save) produced exactly one copy, robot-playtested clean (5 rounds + leaderboard, 0 findings); controlled rerun confirmed no double-save. 1116 tests (18 new).
- 2026-08-14 (per-game setup mode via the Customize flow): **Recipe-born activities now carry setup knobs in the library Customize dialog** — the teacher-requested "choose how many questions when you use Speed Quiz," done the owner's way: customize and set up together in ONE dialog, choices persist into the saved copy, hosting stays zero-click forever after (Customize → Preview → Host; next time just Host). Five pieces. (1) **Provenance stamp**: `compileRecipe` writes `config.recipe = {id, version, params}` (normalized post-default params, deep-cloned, stamped after substitution so templates can't spoof it) — every recipe-born game now knows what made it; `/api/games/revise` carries the stamp across the AI rewrite (`carryRecipeStamp`), save-as-recipe strips it from new templates. (2) **Setup flags in the recipe schema**: `"setup": true` on integer/boolean/enum params, `"setup": {"mode":"count","label":...}` on arrays (slices to the first N); bad shapes rejected with `RECIPE_INVALID_SETUP_FLAG`. quiz-show flags questions (count) + questionTimer + speedBonus. (3) **Multi-phase `$repeat`**: a `"phases"` map (keyPattern → template) emits SEVERAL phases per item — quiz-show now generates a host-paced "The answer was: X! {{qN.barChart}}" reveal beat after every question (its compiled output had NO reveals before), intro timer dropped (host-paced), and `$if`-dropped generated phases finally register for transition rewiring. (4) **Speed Quiz re-authored as a quiz-show compile** of its own 5 questions (same gameplay + reveal beats, ask/reveal ids → q/r, "Question i of n" headers that self-adjust when sliced); phases are drift-guarded to deep-equal a fresh compile of the stamp (`tests/engine/speed-quiz-recipe-born.test.js`) — to change it, change the recipe or the params. (5) **The dialog + landing**: new `screens/shared/setup-knobs.js` (`knobsFor` gates on stamp/recipe/version agreement — any mismatch = knobless dialog exactly as before; `applyKnobs` slices/overrides), knobs render above the AI questions and work when AI questions fail (that path used to skip the dialog entirely), touched knobs recompile via the existing `POST /api/recipes/:id/compile` (untouched NEVER recompiles, so hand-edited stamped copies can't be clobbered), Skip relabels to "Copy with these settings", revise failures fall back to the KNOBBED config; and `saveCopyAndReturn` lands on `/library?highlight=<id>` with the fresh card scrolled + flashed (Preview/Host right there) instead of dumping the teacher in the editor. Verified: robot playtests clean on both the rebuilt Speed Quiz (5 rounds) and a knob-sliced 3-question copy saved through the real API (leaderboard sums exactly 3); CDP screenshots of the dialog (knobs + AI questions) and the highlighted landing. 1098 tests (37 new).
- 2026-08-13 (Speed Quiz editor pass: mark-the-answer, shuffle, score-wiring): the "small things that made editing a quiz harder than it had to be" batch, from the day's field feedback. (1) **✓ correct-answer toggle on every choice row**, in BOTH Simple view (stringListEditor grew an optional `marker` — green ✓/○ per row, tap to mark/unmark, text edits keep the stored answer in sync, removing the marked row clears it) and the sidebar (same semantics; the free-text field remains for `{{ref}}` answers and now tells you to use the toggle). Screenshot-verified on Speed Quiz. (2) **"Shuffle choices for each student" checkbox** on collect-choice — the engine's per-player `shuffle` had existed since the bluff pools but had NO editor UI. (3) **Correct-answer typo trap closed in the validator**: a literal correctAnswer matching no literal choice now warns "nobody can ever be right. Check for a typo or extra spaces" (templated answers/choices exempt). (4) **Leaderboard score-wiring**: `buildDataRefOptions` finally offers `.scores` for graded collect-choice/buzz/estimate-with-answer (the dropdown literally could not select a question's scores before), array `from` renders as a plain "Summing scores from N steps" line instead of a mangled custom option, and a **"Σ Count every scored step" button** sets `from` to every earlier score-producing step — the add-a-question trap (new question's points silently never reaching the board) is now one tap. Also noticed: Simple view already renders `{{ask1.barChart}}` as a "bar chart from step 3" chip, so the announce-clarity feedback is mostly served there. Question-count-at-host-time ("setup mode") deliberately NOT bolted on: the Customize dialog is words-only by design, and per-game setup mode is its own design wave (queued; the quiz-show recipe covers add/remove questions today). 1061 tests (2 new).
- 2026-08-13 (team competition made literal: team-scored leaderboard): **leaderboard.teamsFrom** (optional phaseRef to a team-split, checklist's pattern) rolls individual scores into ranked team totals via pure `engine/phases/team-standings.js` (competition ranking 1-1-3 like the individual board; members keep their own contribution, sorted high-to-low; teamless late-joiners excluded from totals but still see their individual rank; missing/skipped split falls back to the individual board with a warn). Validator: dangling or wrong-type teamsFrom fails loudly (game-loader, mirroring the checklist block — the generic phaseRef machinery does NOT check existence on non-transition fields, learned by test). Host projector leads with team rows + member-contribution lines (`.team-standing-row/-members`); player screen shows "#1. Team Red, 2400 team points (800 from you)" with own-team highlight, whole winning team gets confetti; reconnect path carries teamStandings + myTeam. Editor: "Team totals" select on the leaderboard sidebar (lists team-split steps). **Storyboard: a teams step before a quiz now auto-wires teamsFrom** — the compiler makes it a real team competition deterministically; AI prompt updated (teams+quiz = promisable team totals, honesty rule adjusted) + PHASE_EXTRA_GUIDANCE. Proof: robot playtest logs "Leaderboard: 4 players, 4 teams" and completes clean; live Spanish request now opens with "Each player answers individually, and your team's combined score will determine the winner" — exactly the true mechanic — with 8 correct questions including irregulars. 1059 tests (10 new).
- 2026-08-13 (the Spanish-case fix: quiz + teams storyboard bricks): **The most common teacher request shape (a scored review quiz) is now buildable from Create on the first try.** Reproduced the failed field request ("Jackbox-style team competition reviewing Spanish present/past tense") end to end: recipe match correctly declined, and the storyboard layer produced 3 hardcoded UNGRADED collect/reveal rounds while its intro text promised points and teams it couldn't deliver — root cause: the storyboard grammar knew only 10 of the 28 phase types, with no words for scoring, teams, or leaderboards. Fix: (1) **quiz brick** — `{questions: [{text, choices, correct}], timer?, speedBonus?}` compiles deterministically to the proven Speed Quiz shape (graded collect-choice per question + answer announce with barChart + leaderboard summing every round); per-question validation in plain sentences (correct must exactly match a choice; bad questions drop individually, cap 15). (2) **teams brick** — random team-split, groupSize wins over teamCount, default 4 teams. (3) **Honesty rule in the storyboard prompt**: mechanics exist only where a brick provides them — no text may promise points/winners without a quiz step, never team scores (leaderboard has no team aggregation; queued as an engine feature). (4) Concierge validator (suggest-validate.js STORYBOARD_BRICKS + step sanitizer) carries questions/teamCount/groupSize through; phase-names.js labels. (5) **Approval UI shows every quiz question with its ✓ answer** + per-question ✕ — the teacher fact-check gate for the AI-wrong-facts problem, before anything is built. Proof: same request re-run live → announce/teams(4)/honest-framing announce/8 correct conjugation questions/end → compiled 22 phases, validator clean → **robot playtest completed, zero findings**. 1049 tests (7 new).
- 2026-08-13 (field-feedback quick wins, batch 1 of the day's feedback triage): (1) **Host join line projector-readable**: `.join-instructions` ("Go to .../player and enter this code") and the QR panel's `.join-url` bumped from ~1.1rem to 1.8rem — students read these off the projector, they were caption-sized next to an 8rem room code. (2) **Leaderboard self-highlight was ink-on-ink**: `.leaderboard-highlight` set its text color at (0,1,0) specificity and lost to `#leaderboard-standings p` / `.standings p` text rules, so under the live paste-up theme (accent = ink #221E1C) your own row was black-on-black on BOTH host and player. Fixed by scoping the highlight selectors to outrank the container rules (host + player stylesheets, plus their paste-up border-radius overrides). (3) **Library card order is now Customize/Edit → Preview → Host**: a teacher meeting an activity for the first time previews before hosting, so Host anchors the right edge; it stays visually primary (vermillion). (4) **"Fun" chip no longer near-empty**: visitors only see FEATURED activities and only Speed Quiz carried `energize`, so the ⚡ chip filtered to one card. Featured defaults (repo layer; Neon overrides still win) now include Doodle Bluff, Emoji Movies, and Trivia Bluff — energize count on the public shelf goes 1 → 4. (5) **Recipe #21 Memory Sketch** (draw-from-memory → preview gate → host-paced reveal-one gallery; params: subject promptDeck + draw timer) + new `from-memory` lanyard deck (12 subjects: Winnie the Pooh, a bicycle with pedals and chain, a world map...) — the "draw Winnie the Pooh from memory" teacher request, assembled entirely from existing bricks; recipes-integration compiles + validates it automatically. 1042 tests green.
- 2026-08-11 (Doodle Bluff + the drawing-round primitives): **The Drawful shape is now buildable from bricks** (teacher asked Create for "something like Drawful from Jackbox"; the gap was real: nothing inside a foreach could SHOW a drawing). Engine: (1) new `drawingFrom` field on announce/collect/collect-choice — a data path (headline use `_current.drawing` inside a foreach over drawing responses) resolved at phase enter by `engine/phases/display-drawing.js` and emitted as `displayDrawing`; host and player render it read-only on new canvases (with the recurring `[hidden]`-vs-`display:block` override); never preloads the drawing pad. (2) foreach sub-phase remapping extracted to pure `engine/phases/foreach-remap.js` and EXTENDED to `choicePool[].from` + `excludeAuthored` (the bluff-vote pattern finally works inside rounds), with `{{_current.*}}` now eagerly resolved in `correctAnswer` + choicePool literals too — left to runtime, the per-player `.assigned` machinery intercepted them and the truth on every ballot silently became a placeholder string (caught by the first robot playtest); choicePool entries are copy-on-write since sub-configs are shallow clones (round 2 would have inherited round 1's baked-in truth). (3) foreach scoring extracted to `engine/phases/foreach-scoring.js` + new mode `"scores"`: adopts the sub-phase's own graded map per round, so collect-choice's truth points AND foolPoints accumulate (modes correct/tally lose fool points); validator warns when the target sub-step grades nothing. (4) collect sub-phases now honor foreach self-exclusion like collect-choice always did: the round's artist gets "This one is yours!" instead of the input (they could otherwise plant a decoy title on their own drawing and farm fool points), plus a defensive close-submissions filter. **Game #35 Doodle Bluff** (games/doodle-bluff, unfeatured) + **recipe #20** (params: phrase prompt, rounds, draw time): phrases → rotate-draw ({{phrases.assigned}}) → preview gate (the projector drawing-gate rule) → foreach rounds (title the drawing → bluff-vote with the truth injected via {{_current.assigned}} → host-paced reveal with barChart) → leaderboard. Robot-playtested clean (grading verified against real phrases in the logs) + full CDP prototype tour screenshot-verified (drawing renders on host + players in title/vote/reveal beats; artist correctly locked out). AI guidance: BLUFF ROUNDS pattern added to foreach's PHASE_EXTRA_GUIDANCE. 1042 tests (32 new).
- 2026-08-09 (mid-activity teacher pairing): **The 💻 pairing chip now lives in every phase, not just the lobby** (teacher's question: "in Teacher Is Reviewing, what if they didn't open that already?"). The click-to-reveal PIN chip sat inside the lobby's room-code section, so a teacher who skipped pairing had no way to open the console mid-game — the preview screen's hint pointed at a Teacher view they couldn't reach (the on-projector reveal fallback worked, but defeats the drawing gate). The chip is now a fixed bottom-right corner element (sound toggle owns top-right, brand mark top-left), hidden until `room-created` (restart-rebind re-fires it, so restored rooms keep the chip), with the recurring `[hidden]`-vs-`display:flex` override and a max-width on the info bubble so the URL + room + PIN string wraps on the wall. New `teacherConsolePaired` flag (set on `teacher-console-joined`) drives an adaptive preview hint: unpaired rooms see "No Teacher view open yet? Use the 💻 chip in the corner to pair one on a second device" instead of instructions for a view they don't have. The PIN protections are untouched: two-tap reveal with the students-may-see-this warning, 15s auto-hide, loud console-join announcements. 1010 tests.
- 2026-08-09 (copy round, teacher feedback): homepage promotes the guide ("First time? Read the one-page teacher guide" under the primary cards + Teacher Guide footer link), the guide's console line reads "Open the teacher console" instead of the literal /teacher route, and the setup card stops over-promising: "When you customize an activity, we'll suggest ready-made questions that fit your class" (the suggestion moment is Customize, not the card itself).
- 2026-08-09 (later still — Story Builder rebuilt as everyone-writes): the teacher's catch: the relay-based recipe built ONE story, so in a class of 18 the 16th student did nothing for most of the activity. Rebuilt on the One More Thing rotation pattern: every student writes an opening from the shared seed, then four rotation rounds (`rotateFrom` + `prefillFromAssigned` + `appendOnly`, maxLength growing 240→1200) each add a sentence to a different classmate's story (pass-2 asks for a twist, pass-3 steers toward the ending, last round lands it), then `reveal scope:"own"` returns every finished story to its starter and a `reveal-one` gallery reads a sample aloud (`storiesRead` param → limit, default 5). Params now: `premise` (promptDeck, NEW "story-premises" deck, 10 seeds in the lanyard bank), `writeTime` (75s default), `storiesRead`; relay/order params gone. N students = N stories, everyone writing every round, every story gets five authors. Verified: recipe compiles + validates in the suite, 6-bot robot playtest completed all phases with 0 findings, form + deck picker screenshot-verified. 1010 tests; prompts 299 → 309. teacher's first live use of the new profile hit "Make it yours" asking their subject and grade again. Now the saved class description rides the `customize-questions` request (client → route → `generateCustomizeQuestions(config, classDescription)`), the Haiku prompt gets a "we ALREADY KNOW their class... do not ask about grade level, age, or subject in any form; go one level deeper" block, mock mode mirrors it, and the dialog itself shows "Writing for your class: ..." so nobody wonders whether to repeat it. Live-verified (science profile → "What specific science topic or concept is your class studying right now...?" on both-sides-rope, unit + drawing-prompt questions on art-gallery). 1010 tests (2 new guards: prompt carries the class + forbids re-asking; mock questions never say subject/grade).

(1) **/owner replaces the in-page owner link** — "Show full library (site owner)" read to teachers as content being withheld (reviewer flagged it); the buttons are gone from library + designer grids, and the owner now visits `/owner` (302 → `/library?owner=1`, unlock flow runs, param stripped so refresh doesn't re-prompt). (2) **Teacher profile + first-visit setup card** — new `screens/shared/teacher-profile.js` (localStorage, no accounts: gradeBand + subjects, validated ids only); the library's first visit shows a "New here? Start with this" card (projector + Chromebooks orientation, ~3 min setup, try-Preview nudge, guide link) with grade-band and subject chips; saved profiles collapse to a quiet "Your class: ... · Change" line; skip is honored. (3) **One-page teacher guide at /guide** — the long-owed cheat sheet: what you need, the first five minutes, what you control live (pacing, console + PIN reveal, moderation, drawing gates, mute), a six-row "if this happens, do this" table (code fails, wifi drop, host tab closed, projector moderation, late join, slow AI), privacy in one breath. Linked from the setup card + library footnote. (4) **Subject prompt decks + "for your class" picks** — six new lanyard decks (social studies / ELA / science claims + perspective topics, 38 prompts, tagged `subjects`+`gradeBands`) wired into both-sides-rope and whose-eyes; the deck picker floats profile-matched decks to the top marked "· for your class"; an untouched promptDeck default prefills with a random matching prompt ("Filled in for your class" note, best-effort, choices-carrying prompts excluded); library Customize passes "Their class: Middle school (6-8), Social studies" into the revise request. Screenshot-verified (setup card, guide, prefilled claim, sorted picker); **1008 tests** (10 new: TeacherProfile unit suite + subject-deck id drift guards; teacher-profile.js registered in the XSS sink scan). Prompt count 261 → 299.

Now: Advanced sidebar gains "How many get read" on foreach (under the data-source picker, applies to every pattern) and "How many get shown" on reveal-one (with the keep-empty-for-fairness warning from the schema); the Builder rail inherits both automatically (it hosts the same relocated form). Four escalating facts per person (common → only-me) via multi-field collect, then foreach (shuffle, limit 8) deals one card at a time: five host-paced announce beats reveal facts one by one on the projector while the ROOM plays standing/sitting physically (devices only write the cards; the name appears only in the final beat). Closes with an AI what-we-have-in-common summary behind a preview gate. Design notes: `eliminate` is the wrong tool for this shape (it removes devices from the game; sitting is physical + self-reported), and fact templates must read `{{_current.fields.factN}}` (the fields.* nesting gotcha). Verified by robot playtest (26 phases, 0 findings, tokens resolve, owner named); the UNIVERSAL sim false-alarmed by double-advancing the host-paced announce into the collect (its generic advance-phase carries no phaseInstanceId so the stale-guard can't drop it — real clients echo ids; two-truths only passes it because its announces have timers). Registered in validator-diagnostics; 998 tests.

The cobalt header now has exactly three documented button roles (comment block in editor.css): PRIMARY = solid go-live green, one per bar (▶ Host — Sam suggested blue, but blue-on-cobalt disappears; green matches the projected START! semantic product-wide); ATTENTION = solid yellow, state-driven only (Save while dirty — pure CSS via the `save-status-dirty ~ #save-btn` sibling selector, zero JS changes); QUIET = white-outline ghost (More, Check for Errors, Preview, Save-when-clean — new `.btn-ghost`; the old purple `.btn-review` and same-yellow `.btn-secondary` pills leave the header). `white-space: nowrap` keeps the pills one-line. No behavior changes; no JS referenced the removed classes. Screenshot-verified clean + dirty states at 1440px; 998 tests.

Picker: a proper card (headline "Host an activity", sub-line, uppercase label, design-system-styled select, description, "Browse the full library instead" link) replaces the bare label + native select; the wordmark h1 shrinks to a mark (2rem) so content owns the screen. Lobby: roster is now avatar chips that fill in as the class arrives (rhymes with the student lobby's holding chips; same `Juice.avatarFor`), "Who's in" heading with a live count badge, and a real empty state ("Waiting for students to join..."); the disabled START finally says why ("Start unlocks when the first student joins." at zero; below the activity's minPlayers an informational "Made for N+ students, M in so far." — the gate itself is unchanged: unlocks at 1). Plumbing: the socket `get-games` listing and both `/api/games` paths now carry `minPlayers` (falling back to the lobby phase's value — 2 configs declare it there), and the `?game=` deep-link path calls `updateDesc()` after programmatically selecting (no 'change' event fires, so the description + minPlayers stash read the WRONG game on every library deep link — latent since the deep link shipped). Markup/CSS/copy only, no flow changes; kick buttons and 2026-07-27 lobby behaviors intact. Screenshot-verified all three states (picker, empty lobby, 2-in lobby); 998 tests + full chaos suite green.

Plain-language page in the design system: shortlist card (no accounts, activity-lifetime data, names never to AI, nothing sold/trained-on), the full retention table the compliance review requires (names every room-snapshot content: responses, drawings, player names, reconnect tokens, teacher PIN; plus activity_runs, user games/recipes, feedback with its anonymous-by-design note), AI section with the defensible pii-scrub claim + the honest limit (student-typed text is the answer; teacher moderation is the control), device-local storage, security posture. Linked from home footer + library footnote; route `app.use('/privacy')`. **Two yellow placeholders remain (operator name, privacy contact email) — fill before pushing to prod.** COMPLIANCE-TODO §1 updated. Also the field test's a11y batch: the `--focus-ring` token finally has consumers (global `:focus-visible` rule in design.css for button/a/input/textarea/select — student and host buttons rode browser-default focus; page-specific rules still win by load order), mic button 30px→40px hit area at 0.55 opacity (touchscreen Chromebook minimum; glyph unchanged, input padding 36→44px), and the detached library-search mic fixed generically (attachMic transfers the input's computed max-width onto the wrapper, so a capped input inside a full-width flex row keeps its mic in ITS corner). Screenshot-verified; 998 tests.
- 2026-08-08 (library findability): **Subject searches stop dead-ending** (field-test backlog #2: Maria's first instinct was searching "history" — zero matches, because the activities are topic-agnostic shells and nothing said so). Three parts: (1) new optional top-level `keywords` config field (added to `GAME_CARD_META_FIELDS`, so both listing paths carry it; phase-level allow-list untouched) seeded into the 12 public-facing configs with subject/topic terms ("history", "debate", "vocabulary", "think pair share", "any subject"...) — "history" now finds Both Sides of the Rope + Whose Eyes; searches match keywords in BOTH the library and the designer grid. (2) **Search rescue**: a query with zero hits no longer renders "No matches" — instead an honest note ("Nothing is named 'X', and that's okay: these activities work with any subject...") + the concierge link above the full shelf (goal filter still respected). (3) `?q=` deep link lands on the library with a search applied. Bonus: group-work-day finally has tags (group work/stations/projects — it was invisible to every chip). NOTE: the thin Connect chip is partly live featured-override curation in Neon (one-voice flipped off) — a DB decision deliberately not touched here. Screenshot-verified both states; 998 tests.
- 2026-08-08 (holding screens): **Waits show the room, not a void** — the field test's #1 convergent finding (all three testers; Sam's "one big design move" = Jayden's "one change") shipped as a holding treatment across every player wait. Server: two new room-wide events — `room-roster` (lobby ONLY: names + count; the projected host roster already makes this public, but mid-game the roster stays off student devices) broadcast from join/reconnect/disconnect/grace-expiry, and `room-progress` (**counts only, never names** — submission status on a student device must not create who's-slow pressure) broadcast from submit-response and submit-vote. Player: lobby shows "N of us here" + avatar chips (Juice.avatarFor, pop-in animation) as classmates join; submitted + vote-submitted screens show a live "X of Y in" line + what-happens-next copy; process/game-waiting screens get pulsing holding dots (reduced-motion safe); stale counts reset at each game-started/vote-start. Copy: "AI is working on something special..." → "Reading everyone's answers..." (Jayden: "homework in a party hat"). Verified by new `scripts/simulate-holding.js` (7 checks: roster completeness, counts-only payload keys, every-player delivery, lobby-only roster) + 420px screenshot of the live lobby; 998 tests + late-join sim still green.
- 2026-08-08 (persona field test + fix wave): **Three agent testers** (history teacher / bored student / creative front-end dev) ran the live build independently; report published as an artifact, full texts in session transcript. Four fixes shipped from the ranked list: (1) **Late-join stranding** — the join-room NEW-player branch never called `sendCurrentState` (only reconnects got it), so a student joining mid-game stared at lobby copy AND silently blocked every all-submitted auto-advance (the eligible count included them). Two testers hit it independently in live rooms. New joins now drop into the current phase (onReconnect handlers already tolerate never-seen players: collect offers the input, merge/vote/team-split show contextual waits), wrapped so a failure degrades to the waiting screen, never a failed join. Verified by new `scripts/simulate-late-join.js` (7 checks incl. late submit counted 1/4) + full chaos suite green. (2) **Escaped em dashes** — 6 student-facing `'—'` literals (player + host leaderboards/winner attribution) dodged the no-em-dash scanner two ways: the file-level `includes('—')` short-circuit never saw a file whose only dashes were escapes, and the raw-slice token check can't decode them. Strings fixed (`Name: 50 pts`, `by Maya`); scanner now checks acorn's DECODED token values + escape-aware short-circuit, with escaped-form self-tests. (3) **Customize revise envelope** — Sonnet sometimes returns the revised config BARE (valid JSON, no `{updatedConfig, summary}` wrapper) → "AI response missing updatedConfig" → every library Customize silently degraded to a plain copy. Now a top-level object with `phases` is accepted as the config; prompt states the envelope rule explicitly; max_tokens 4096→8192 (full-config responses truncated). Verified with a real API call on the field test's exact failing scenario (whose-eyes + "Reconstruction / 11th grade": 200, 6 phases, real summary). (4) **Concierge params clamp to recipe min/max** — `suggest-validate.js` accepted any number for a known param name, so the AI's minutes (`evidenceTime: 4`) landed in seconds fields (min 60) and create failed with an error the teacher didn't cause. Numeric params now clamp into `[min, max]` (non-numeric values for numeric specs dropped), and the suggest prompt lists numeric params with label + range ("Evidence time (seconds), 60-300") so the model fills legal values in the first place. **998 tests** (7 new). Remaining field-test backlog (wait-state holding component, library subject-search dead-end, privacy page, editor header hierarchy, host pre-room styling, mic touch-target) not started.

(1) **Builder's "Browse all steps →" did nothing visible**: it expanded the LEFT palette — hidden behind the popover the teacher was looking at — and palette tiles insert at the END anyway, not into the opened gap. Now the popover itself expands into the full grouped step list ("Pick any step", same palette groups/colors) and picking one inserts into THAT gap; "← Back to suggestions" returns. (2) **Simple's "Add a Step" showed 18 of 28 types**: `PHASE_CATEGORIES` predated turn/merge/one-voice/buzz/estimate/match/sort/checklist. Rebuilt as ONE taxonomy with the Builder palette (same verb groups: Ask/Show/Decide/Team up/Rounds/AI) covering every type incl. the Secret + Clue brick — `addPhaseOfType` now prefers `StepSuggestions.defaultPhaseFor` (certified hostable defaults; turns collect-two into its real two-field collect) before the per-type default chain. PHASE_CATALOG gained the missing merge/one-voice/collect-two entries. Screenshot-verified: gap popover full list, Simple picker all groups, Secret+Clue insert producing `{type:collect, fields:2}`. 973 tests.

The template picker and 5 content templates are gone; every one had a recipe twin (Simple Poll→class-poll, Quiz Show→quiz-show, Elimination Game→elimination-tournament, Creative Writing→story-builder/creative-vote) EXCEPT Question & Share, whose deliberately zero-AI shape (Marcus's finding — raw answers on the projector, no summarize step) is now **`recipes/question-share.json` (19th recipe)**: params question + timer, compiles to the template's exact lobby→intro→ask→share→end chain (live-compiled, zero AI verified). Create page side doors simplify to "Browse recipes · Start from scratch" (blank-into-editor). `templates.js` survives blank-only for the editor's createBlankConfig and is no longer loaded by the Create page. "Show me a complete example" is covered better by library Customize (clone any built-in). 973 tests green (recipe-schema suite validates the new recipe automatically).

"Build your own activity" now navigates directly to /designer; the question is asked exactly once, in the idea box with the mic. The builder-request signal still files quietly on the way (fixed message, no note field), and the lanyard-builder localStorage flag still sets. Screenshot-verified: one click → Create an Activity.

Removed: the no-match view's "Generate Custom (Advanced)" button, the whole legacy client flow (showLegacyAIGenerateModal/fetchClarifyQuestions/createFromAI, 280 lines), routes `POST /api/games/generate` + `/api/games/generate-questions` (now 404), and AIService.generateQuestions/generateGame/_generateGameMock/_generateGameReal/_autoPolishConfig + CLARIFY_QUESTIONS_PROMPT (224 lines). KEPT deliberately: `GAME_GENERATOR_PROMPT` and `_fixGeneratedConfig` — the Ask-AI revise flows (whole-game + per-step) build on them; `/api/games/generate-theme` untouched. Custom creation is now recipe match or storyboard-only — AI writes words, deterministic compileStoryboard writes structure. No-match buttons: Cancel / Pick from Recipes / Plan it step by step. 973 tests green; live-verified (endpoint 404, clean no-match dialog).
- 2026-08-07 (evening): **Library doorway carries your idea into Create** (teacher's catch: dictate your idea into the Build-your-own note, land on Create an Activity, and have to say it all again). "Start building" now rides a real note (10+ chars, the Create page's own launch threshold) through the existing `?idea=` deep link — the idea lands IN the box and the from-description flow auto-launches; shorter/empty notes go to plain /designer as before. The builder-request feedback signal still files either way; placeholder copy now says both destinations honestly. Flow-verified: dialog → typed idea → landed on Create with the matcher already running.
- 2026-08-07 (even later): **Mic became a corner icon + library coverage** (teacher feedback: "transparent icon in the corner of text boxes", and the library's Build-your-own dialog had no mic). attachMic redesigned: the input moves into a `position:relative` `.mic-wrap` (same node — every existing reference/handler keeps working; flex-row parents detected and the wrapper takes over the flexing) and the mic renders as a quiet transparent 🎤 absolutely positioned top-right INSIDE the box (opacity .4, hover/live full; input gets paddingRight so text never runs under it; blocked mic = dimmed `.mic-off`, no text swap). `enableFocusMics` (focus-delegation) replaced by **`Speech.autoAttach()`**: eager initial sweep + MutationObserver, so every text box — present or rendered later — visibly carries the icon (student answer box, multi-field inputs, idea box, Simple/Builder/Ask AI, recipe forms, dialogs, search). Library page now loads the module + autoAttach — the Build-your-own doorway note box (and search) speak too. Player's `#response-input[hidden] + .mic-btn` hide rule survives (order preserved inside the wrapper). Screenshot-verified: library doorway dialog, Create idea row (flex intact), editor rail, live player collect. Debug note for the record: a screenshot-profile port collision resurrected an old profile's `lanyardEditorBuilder=1` and opened the editor in Builder view — looked like a regression, was stale localStorage in the CDP profile. 973 tests.
- 2026-08-07 (later still): **Mic input reaches the teacher side — talk your activity into existence.** The Create page's idea box gets a full-size "🎤 Speak instead" button in the input row (speak the idea, hit Make it), and both /designer and the editor run new `Speech.enableFocusMics()`: a focusin-delegation mode where ANY text field the teacher focuses grows a compact mic — Simple view step boxes, the Builder rail's primary-text field, Ask AI, recipe param forms, storyboard cards — with zero per-form wiring, so future forms get dictation for free (opt an area out with a `.no-mic` ancestor class; re-renders self-heal since buttons die with their fields and re-attach on next focus). Mic button styles moved to shared/design.css (one definition: `--theme-*` vars win on themed player screens, design tokens fall back on teacher chrome). Verified: 973 tests, Create page screenshot (idea row reads [box] [🎤 Speak instead] [MAKE IT]), editor screenshot with the compact mic under a focused field (headless gotcha for the record: programmatic `.focus()` fires no focusin without real window focus — synthetic FocusEvent dispatch proves the listener; real clicks work).
- 2026-08-07 (later): **Mic input — speak instead of typing.** New shared module `screens/shared/speech-input.js` (browser global `Speech` + side-effect-importable, the bot-brain pattern): `attachMic(inputEl)` renders a "🎤 Speak instead" button using the browser's built-in Web Speech API — present on Chrome/Chromebooks, feature-detected so unsupported browsers never see the button, and a blocked mic (school-managed devices) flips it to a disabled "Mic unavailable" instead of erroring. Dictation REBUILDS on top of whatever was typed when the mic opened (pure `mergeTranscript`, unit-tested — typing is never overwritten), shows interim results live, respects the input's maxLength, and fires real input events so the char counter and notices react. One dictation at a time; leaving a screen (`showSection`) or submitting stops it. Wired to the player collect textarea (button hides with it in choice/drawing/fields modes via a sibling CSS rule) and to every multi-field input (compact per-field mics). Prototype player iframes gained `allow="microphone"` so teacher previews can use it. Privacy shape: NO audio ever reaches the game server — recognition runs through the browser's own speech service (Google on Chromebooks) and only the final TEXT enters the normal submit path, where the content filter treats it exactly like typed text. Not yet wired: relay input + merge draft (same helper would drop in). 972 tests (9 new); screenshot-verified on the collect screen.
- 2026-08-07: **The crown shows what they won for** — the winner phase now surfaces the winning submission itself, not just a name and a number. Engine: pure `traceEntryRef` (explicit `entryFrom` override, else auto-traced winner `from` → vote phase → its `candidates` responses ref; literal candidate lists refused — no playerIds) + `findWinnerEntries` (each winner's own text; drawings/blanks skipped) in winner-handler.js; `_runWinner` attaches `winnerEntry`/`winnerEntries` to the phase output (so `{{crown.winnerEntry}}` works in templates — schema output fields feed the grammar) and the emit. Corn Story + Elimination Game get it with zero config changes. Fun: host screen plays a build-up beat — "And the winner is…" pulses over a new `drumroll` juice cue (one accelerating-tom score, so the per-cue throttle can't chop it) — then the crown lands: 👑 + avatar + name slam, fanfare + confetti, the winning entry on an accent-bordered quote card (ties show every tied entry with authors), staggered standings. Player screens mirror the beat quietly (sound/confetti still only on the winner's own device, "That's you!" detail line). Auto-advance pause default 5s → 10s — the crown is a payoff beat. Editor: "What they won for" dropdown (auto by default) + entry element in both live previews; `entry` toggle in hostShow/playerShow. Verified: 11 new unit tests (963), live socket probe (new `scripts/simulate-winner-entry.js` + `games/_winner-entry` fixture — entry traced, delivered to host and player, pause 10), elimination-game robot playtest (pre-existing eliminate-family stall quirk only), screenshot of the reveal state.

Unfeatured. Registered in the validator snapshot; robot-playtested clean (4 bots, 0 findings).
- 2026-08-03 (later still): **Decorative-scoring architecture nets + structural needs-work batch.** The two-truths defect was a CLASS, not a config: the validator checked refs/types but never "can this scoring ever award a point", and the robot playtest asserted completion but not that scores were nonzero. Three nets now: (1) validator `SCORING_NEVER_AWARDS` warning — all-zero tally pointMaps AND leaderboard/winner reading `.scores` from a phase with no scoring configured (fixtures in validator-diagnostics); (2) robot playtest emits an ERROR when a leaderboard shows with every score 0 despite bots answering everything; (3) AI-generation guidance teaches the idiom (capture the secret in its own field, never zero pointMaps, every guess loop needs a host-paced reveal beat). Structural fixes: **elimination-game** rebuilt Corn-Story-style (3 unrolled escalating rounds, eliminated players become the voting JURY, framing announces); **scamper** cut 7 lenses → 4, per-lens intro announces removed, default problem on-screen, and the payoff is now `reveal scope:"own"` — every student sees their idea's full journey; **mad-lib-mashup** lost the fake AI chaos-score leaderboard, gained teacher previews before both AI reveals + an AI shortlist so the final vote is 6 options not 25; **dream-vacation** ("Pitch" now) ranks an AI-merged shortlist of 6 and caps the relay at 8 turns (`turns` already existed); **charades-bowl** got `turn.poolLimit` (new engine field — round 1 draws ≤30 phrases, stores the draw as `.pool`, rounds 2/3 ref `round1.pool` to keep Fishbowl's same-bowl mechanic); **convince-me** reframed as write-then-optionally-read-aloud (host-paced spotlight), blameless rating labels; **punchline-showdown** generates 15 prompts (max pairs) + odd-player judge framing; **yes-or-no-bets** got a question worth betting on; **emoji-movies/class-quiz/who-said-it** surface the round's guesses/vote distribution in their reveals via existing sub-phase ref remapping (`{{guess.responses.list}}` / `{{guess.barChart}}` — no engine change needed). All sims pass; 952 tests. Known pre-existing sim quirk: eliminate-family games show a stall+force-skip in the robot playtest (corn-story control run reproduces it).
- 2026-08-03 (later): **Needs-work fixes: the scoring-promise cluster.** Two new bluffing primitives on collect-choice: **`foolPoints`** (`engine/phases/bluff-scoring.js`, pure + tested — the AUTHOR of a fake earns N points per classmate who picks it; merged into `.scores` alongside correctAnswer grading; needs excludeAuthored for authorship) and **`poolLimit`** (per-player ballot cap — samples the `from` entries, ALWAYS keeps injected literals like the truth/house lie; a 25-fake ballot on a 30s timer was unreadable). Games: **two-truths-a-lie REBUILT** — the root defect was that the game never learned which statement was the lie (one text box, all-zero pointMap, winner crowned off a zero board); now a multi-field collect (truth1/truth2/lie) captures the lie by construction, `_current.shuffledFields` + `mode:"correct"` scoring pays lie-spotters 100, a host-paced reveal-lie beat gives the author the mic, and the AI class-portrait is told which parts are invented. **definition-bluff + trivia-bluff** finally deliver their long-promised scoring (100 truth-pick + 50/fool, flat not speed), gained leaderboards, confession lines in the reveals, poolLimit 8, and honest end copy. **human-vs-ai** got its missing reveal-ai beat ("{{aiPosition}} was the AI — what gave it away?") + a discussion-hook ending. All four robot-playtested (fool-points and real per-iteration foreach scores verified in the logs). 950 tests.
- 2026-08-03: **Durable featured curation** (`featured_overrides` Neon table) — the owner ★ toggle used to PUT the whole config back onto Render's ephemeral disk, so built-in featured flips silently reverted on every deploy (teacher hit this after the coherence-wave pushes). Now: repo `featured` flags are the DEFAULTS; a new owner-gated `POST /api/games/:id/featured` writes built-in flips to Neon (user games keep the flag in their durable config; local dev without the branch-relevant conditions falls back to writing the config file — disk is durable there). Both game listings (`/api/games` + socket `get-games`) merge overrides via pure `engine/featured-merge.js` (`applyFeaturedOverrides`, tested) and carry `featuredDefault`, so the owner card shows a • drift marker with a "saved override in effect" title — drift is visible, never silent. Flipping a game back to its repo default DELETES the override (storage: "default") so the repo regains control. Override read failures fall back to repo defaults (pickers can't be taken down by a DB blip). Also: prototype page back link returns to the editor when opened from it (`from=editor`, closes the preview tab if the editor tab still exists). 942 tests.

Engine: **`collect.appendOnly`** (`engine/phases/append-only.js` + server-side combine — the inherited list renders read-only and the server rebuilds responses from its own copy of the assigned text, so a vandal structurally cannot gut a classmate's list; empty additions legal so timer auto-submit passes the list through) and **rotation collects stamp `assigned` onto response records** at close (`{{_current.assigned}}` now works in reveal-one itemTemplates). Games: both-sides-rope (what-ifs reveal inserted before the re-vote — they can finally move minds; delta decluttered), whose-eyes (retype-the-viewpoint field REMOVED — also fixed the circle's silently-blank tokens: multi-field values live under `fields.*`, the old template read top-level; honest playTime), one-more-thing (appendOnly on both add rounds, thin-list rescue line, quiet-minute hostTemplate on the returned reveal), one-voice (ending honest when target unreached, big-class expectation line), someones-got-you (honest pass copy + stuck-writer fallback line), snowball (day-one framing, decide→connect tag), weekend-poem (poem scales ~1 line per 2 students with near-verbatim weaving, preview gate before reveal, "whose weekend can you spot?" beat), art-gallery (guess-aloud gallery beat, imported-joke intro fixed, hide-don't-reject preview nudge, student-facing ending), closer (all 9 share screens now say "turn to your partner — tell them what stood out", partner-name expectations, t1q1 60s, playTime ~30-40 min). New invariant sim `scripts/simulate-append-only.js` (vandal-cannot-gut + circle-shows-viewpoint). 935 tests; all 9 kit games robot-playtested clean.

(3) **Payoff phases un-timed** (16 timers removed across 13 configs): final reveals, leaderboards, and per-round author/answer reveals are host-paced — the teacher rides the laugh instead of a 5s timer steamrolling it. Rule: payoff beats are host-paced, never timed. (4) **speed-quiz earns its featured slot**: 5 unrolled questions (loopBack can't vary content), summed leaderboard, mojibake glyph fixed. All 19 touched games verified end-to-end via robot playtest (services/simulator.js, mock mode); 930 tests. (1) **Two new Builder bricks**: `collect-two` (Secret + clue — two-field collect, first hidden until reveal) and `guessing-rounds` (buildGuessingRounds — THE party-game shape: foreach over the last collect, show clue → everyone types a guess → reveal secret + author; pair-aware refs when the source is secret+clue). Surfaced in the palette (Ask/Rounds groups) and suggested after any collect. (2) **compileStoryboard** (pure, in step-suggestions.js): AI proposes a SEQUENCE OF BRICKS with words — never config JSON; the compiler assembles it through the same validated builders the + buttons use, auto-appends the wrap-up, and reports problems as plain sentences. (3) **POST /api/games/storyboard** — Sonnet gets the brick vocabulary and the teacher's description, returns the storyboard (budget-guarded; no student data; no-decorative-emoji rule in prompt). (4) **Approval UI on the Create page**: the no-match view's primary option is now "Plan it step by step" — the storyboard renders as editable step cards ("Nothing exists until you do"), Build compiles client-side, saves, and opens the Builder. **Proof: the teacher's exact Emoji Movies description → AI chose announce → collect-two ("Movie Title"/"Emoji Description") → guessing-rounds → end → compiled with 0 problems → validated 0 errors → robot playtest completed, 0 findings.** AI writes words; deterministic code owns structure. 922 tests (10 new).

(described by Y)" reveal. Uses {{_current.fields.*}} multi-field template refs + foreach playerName normalization. Verified by the REAL robot playtest (services/simulator.js — all 4 clue rounds played, 0 findings); the universal sim script mishandles timer-less announces (buffered-event misalignment, logged as a known sim limitation — the game is fine, the crude sim isn't). Marcus: built a runnable activity in one sitting, palette taxonomy and pretend-players mode praised; flagged silent edit loss (twice), no open-question template, Simple-view dead-ends, jargon validation errors, Simple/Builder step-name mismatches. **Fixed same day:** (1) projected lobby now says "Go to ⟨host⟩/player and enter this code" under the room code; (2) editor header gained **▶ Host** (validating save → /host with the activity loaded); (3) /prototype is now titled **Preview** everywhere (editor button + home footer renamed), auto-launches when an activity is pre-selected, and shows a pick-and-launch hint instead of a blank stage; (4) **beforeunload warning** when the editor has unsaved changes; (5) Simple view's stale "insert data in Advanced" placeholder reworded. Remaining findings logged in SURFACES-PLAN §usability backlog. Agent test artifacts cleaned up (8 test games deleted).
- 2026-08-01: **"All settings →" leads to the Builder, not the stranded canvas** (teacher's catch: after the Advanced demotion, Simple view's per-step "Advanced settings →" link dumped you on a surface the pill no longer names — no context, no way back but the menu). Simple's link (relabeled "All settings →") and the review-panel/validation deep-link path (`selectPhase` from Simple) now call `window.__enterBuilder(stepId)` — the Builder opens with that step selected, its card scrolled into view, and the full settings form in the rail; the technical canvas remains the fallback only if the Builder failed to load. Subtle race fixed en route: enterBuilder's Activity-tab reset runs an ASYNC deselect that nulled a selection landing right after it, so a targeted entry now skips the reset entirely (the target parameter exists precisely because of that race). Screenshot-verified: Simple → All settings lands selected in Builder.
- 2026-08-01: **Advanced demoted to "Technical view" + Builder gains reorder/delete** (teacher's call: with Simple and Builder, Advanced stops being a peer). The editor pill is now **Simple | Builder**; Advanced lives under More ▾ as "Technical view" (same element id, so every existing wiring — simple-view's toggle, Builder's programmatic entry, review-panel deep links — still works; kept as the escape hatch where every possible config is guaranteed visible, revisit after field tests whether it can go entirely). Builder step cards grew a quiet hover tool cluster: **↑ ↓** call new pure `StepSuggestions.moveStep` (pointer surgery on the next-chain only — branch fields like approveNext/nextByWinner untouched; refuses lobby/end/illegal moves; 4 new tests incl. validator pass after moves) and **✕** confirms then reuses the editor's own `deletePhase` (full re-link logic). Found+fixed: leaving Builder re-appended the settings sidebar on the WRONG side (appendChild vs insertBefore-firstChild). 917 tests; screenshot-verified pill, menu, live reorder, and Technical view round-trip.
- 2026-08-01: **The Builder — the mockup became a real editor mode** (teacher: "let's build it"; SURFACES-PLAN Phase 3, designed across six mockup rounds same day). New **Builder** pill between Simple and Advanced: left = step palette (curated v1 set, click-to-add, grouped Ask/Show/Decide/AI); middle = the activity as plain-English cards with a **+** in every gap — ranked next-step suggestions from `screens/shared/step-suggestions.js` (pure module: transition frequencies mined from the built-in configs, arc detection, hostable-as-is defaults; every suggested step is validator-certified by test); right = rail with Activity/Step tabs hosting the RELOCATED real panels (#settings-panel + the portable #phase-config-form/live-preview — zero duplicated forms, every existing edit path intact) plus a Builder-owned primary-text field (prompt/message/template/instruction, live card sync). The whole mockup journey works: empty canvas opens "What happens first?" (announce most common — 33 of 40 built-ins), momentum (clicked suggestions land selected with the next + open), AI tiles swap to the flavor question and land as an ai-process+reveal PAIR with pre-written instructions, "Wrap it up" joins once the arc exists, and the finish panel (Try with pretend players / Host live / Keep polishing) appears when arc+end are both true (a blank lobby+end activity correctly does NOT count as finished). Integration trick: entering Builder clicks the Advanced pill first so simple-view's selectPhase wrapper never flips views; found+fixed: the editor's click-outside-collapse deselected every Builder click (re-render detaches the clicked tile, so closest() failed — guard is the body class). Verified: 913 tests (21 new incl. hostable-as-is enforcement; xss scanner extended to both new files), screenshot review of all states, and a robot playthrough of an activity assembled purely from suggestions (0 errors). Simple view untouched and still the default.
- 2026-08-01: **Surfaces Phase 1 — the duplicate shelf is gone** (docs/SURFACES-PLAN.md; teacher-approved: one-shelf merge, before field tests). `/designer` is now the **Create page**: idea box + Browse recipes + Start from scratch, retitled "Create an Activity", NO activity grid (the grid code in designer.js is element-gated and awaits a pruning pass). The **library is the one shelf**: it gained everything the designer grid uniquely had — Delete for this device's activities (shipped same day), owner mode (unlock link at the grid bottom, owner bar with feedback-inbox link, ★ featured toggles on cards, built-in edit/delete when unlocked). Flows re-pointed: editor "Back to List" → "Back to Library" (always /library; the from=library param hack removed at the HTML level), prototype back link → /library, home footer "Activity Designer" → "Create an Activity", library doorway button → "Start building" (still files the builder-request signal). Mental model: **find it in the Library, start it in Create, shape it in the Editor.** Verified: 890 tests, screenshots of the Create page (recipe picker still opens), library owner link, delete on own cards.
- 2026-08-01: **Card declutter — player-count and step-count badges removed** (library + designer grids). The player range said "roughly a whole class" on every card, and step counts are engine internals a teacher can't act on — play time already answers the real question ("does this fit the period?"). Cards now show time + goal tags + actions. `formatClassSize` helper deleted with its only caller; configs keep their `classSize`/`minPlayers` metadata (still used by validation and the host screen).
- 2026-08-01: **Hover tooltips on every ambiguous button** (user request: "Bot Fill" meant nothing until you clicked it). Native `title` attributes — reliable in tight toolbars, keyboard-discoverable, zero gameplay risk — added across: prototype controls (Launch / Bot Fill / Reset / carousel arrows), editor header (Simple/Advanced toggle, Ask AI, Save as Recipe, Check for Errors, Prototype Mode, Save, Generate Colors, Show Preview), host lobby (Open the room, Copy join link, QR) + preview Approve/Reject + one-voice speaker toggle, teacher console (Start activity, Approve/Try again, Close submissions, Next step, per-entry Hide/Kick with their consequences spelled out), library/designer card Host + Edit buttons, and the designer's "Make it". Tips say what happens next in plain words ("Auto-fill an answer for every practice player so you can move through the steps quickly"), matching the continue-labels philosophy: buttons say what they do.

- 2026-07-27: **Chain primitives — write, pass, add one, get it back** (COMPARATIVE-ADVANTAGE §7 asks 1-3). (1) **`prefillFromAssigned` on collect** — with rotateFrom, the classmate's item lands IN the recipient's text box so they add to it (accumulating lists, the +1-routine move); reconnect-safe; paired with a new **`maxLength`** collect field (default 280 stands; accumulating chains raise it) threaded through server validation (`checkSubmission`) and the player counter/maxlength. (2) **Return-to-author reveal** — `reveal scope:"own"` + `chainFrom:[...]`: every rotation chain returns to the student who started it. The rotation now stores `assignedFrom` (recipient→sender links) on the source phase, and pure `engine/phases/chain-reveal.js` walks the links (`buildChainViews`) — link-based, not index-replay, so reconnect id-migration keeps chains intact (chaos-verified: 3 mid-game reconnects, chains still resolved). `chainDisplay: steps|final` for chain-poem vs accumulating-list rendering; projector shows only a neutral line (chains are private to their authors). Live-probed: 3 players, each got back exactly their own list with both classmates' additions. (3) **`merge groupSize:3`** — trios, the canonical consulting-protocol size (n%3===2 → one pair; n%3===1 → the leftover joins a trio; never a singleton). New game #31 + 18th recipe: **One More Thing** — from-memory recall → two pass-and-add hops → your list comes back grown → AI builds the class's collective memory. Verified: 13 new unit tests (chain walker, trio grouping), universal sim, chaos run, scamper/snowball/finish-my-drawing regression sims. Test count: **882**.
- 2026-07-27: **The thesis slate — the front door now argues what the engine argues** (from docs/COMPARATIVE-ADVANTAGE.md + the DeKoven/New Games and Project Zero research sweeps; the diagnosis: every distinctive primitive was used exactly once while `leaderboard` appeared 18×, so the library said "nicer Kahoot" while the engine said addressed/chained/shared-fate). - 2026-07-27: **Teacher console wave — the console can actually run the class now** (top item of the second external UI review). The headline was a real bug with a tidy root cause: the console's very first "Next step" (and any pre-start action) was silently swallowed because the join snapshot reported `phaseInstanceId: 0` while a lobby room's counter is still `undefined` — the stale-event guard read the mismatch as staleness and dropped it. Fix: no instance id yet → nothing can be stale (`isStalePhaseEvent`). On top of that: (1) **Lobby state** — the console now shows a live "N students joined" count + name list (new `teacher-roster` event on join/leave/grace-expiry; snapshot seeds it) and an explicit **Start activity** button (start-game was already console-gated correctly); the generic next-step is hidden in the lobby so an accidental tap can't start the class. (2) **Descriptive step control** — the console's advance button reuses wave 2's continue-labels ("Send the question to students ▸") via `continueLabel` on `teacher-phase` + the snapshot. (3) **Pairing is visible** — every console join announces itself: "📱 Teacher device connected" chip on the host screen (with device count) and a warning line on already-connected consoles ("if that wasn't you, a student may have the PIN"). (4) **PIN reveal hardened** — the projector chip is now a two-step confirm ("⚠ Students may see this — tap again"), auto-hides after 15s (or 6s if unconfirmed), and hides immediately once a console pairs. Verified by a 7-assertion live probe (roster, labels, console-started game, dual-device notices), the full teacher-console invariant sim, and the complete chaos suite (47 genuinely-stale events still dropped). 868 tests green.
- 2026-07-26: **UI review wave 2 — a library you can actually find things in, and buttons that say what they do** — (1) **Library controls** on the designer grid: search (name/description/tags), goal chips built from a fixed teacher-intent vocabulary (🤝 Connect / 🎨 Create / 💬 Discuss / 🗳️ Decide / 🪞 Reflect / ⚡ Energize / 📚 Review — only goals present in the visible list render; seeded as plain `tags` into ~14 configs so recipes/AI matching can reuse them), ♥ favorites and a "Recently used" section (any card action files it; cap 8) — both localStorage, the same no-accounts model as MyGames. Sections render each activity exactly once: Favorites → Recently used → My Activities → Built-in. Delete visibility now computed per game (own creations always, built-ins owner-only) instead of per section. One render entry point (`refreshLibrary()`) replaces the four scattered `renderGames(applyVisibility(...))` call sites. (2) **Descriptive continue buttons**: announce and reveal host buttons now say what happens NEXT — "Send the question to students", "Start the voting", "Start counting together" — via pure `engine/phases/continue-labels.js` (label map keyed by next phase type, "Continue" fallback, virtual foreach sub-phases covered because they're injected into the same phase map). Live-probed (one-voice intro announce carries "Start counting together") + story-quest/snowball/weekend-poem sims + dedicated Snowball invariant sim all green. Test count: **868** (+4).
- 2026-07-26: **UI review wave 1 — the projector never lies about participation again** — an external UI review of the live site found one real runtime bug and a cluster of first-touch UX problems. Both handlers now send `count: 0, total` using the same eligibility math as the submit handler (foreach author self-exclusion, unpaired-player exclusion); live-probed (0 of 2 before any submission on art-gallery) + Closer sim + universal sims green. (2) **Activity cards are actionable**: the designer grid's cards were clickable divs whose only visible action was Delete and whose click meant "edit" — the wrong default for "I have 10 minutes, run this." Cards now carry real link/button actions — primary ▶ HOST (`/host?game=` auto-creates the room), Edit, Try it (prototype), Delete (own/owner only) — keyboard-focusable with visible focus rings; card-level click removed (no nested-interactive a11y trap). (3) **Home hierarchy**: hosting is the dominant card; building demoted. (4) **Player join form**: permanent ROOM CODE / YOUR NAME labels replace the clipped 46-char placeholder; placeholders grayed. (5) **Teacher console discoverability** (the reviewer toured the whole site and never found /teacher): host-screen chip relabeled "📱 Teacher controls on your phone" with the full open-this-URL + room + PIN instruction on tap, and a Teacher Console link in the home footer. (6) **a11y**: the 🔊 chip announces "Turn sound effects on/off". Waves 2-3 (library search/recents, descriptive continue labels, goal chips, AI storyboard, pinned controls, collective visualizations) captured in NEXT-STEPS §1.2. 864 tests green.
