PUBLIC: visitors see a curated set of 8 featured activities (variety pack: speed-quiz, vocab-match, snowball, one-voice, story-quest, art-gallery, group-work-day, who-said-it — `featured: true` in each config, surfaced through both `/api/games` and the socket `games-list`) plus anything created on their own device (`screens/shared/my-games.js` remembers created ids in localStorage; every save/create path registers). One pure filter (`screens/shared/game-visibility.js`, tested) drives all three pickers — designer grid, host select, prototype — with `?game=` deep links still working for hidden activities. OWNER: `SITE_PASSWORD` was REPURPOSED from "lock all teacher surfaces" to the owner's password — the old broad gate is gone (the public hosting/building IS the product decision), and it now guards only the feedback inbox, `/api/owner-check`, and edits/deletes of built-in activities (PUT/DELETE on a built-in without it → 401; visitor-created activities stay freely editable). "Site owner? Show everything" on the designer grid unlocks owner mode (basic-auth prompt once, browser caches the credential), revealing every activity + per-card ★ featured toggles + delete buttons on built-ins + a link to the inbox. FEEDBACK: floating 💬 widget (home/designer/editor/prototype; createElement/textContent only — scanner-proof) posts anonymous feedback (category + message, no name/email fields BY DESIGN) to `POST /api/feedback` — validated + content-filtered (`engine/feedback-validate.js`), per-IP rate-limited 5/min (`engine/simple-rate-limit.js`, pure + injected clock), stored in a new Neon `feedback` table (ndjson file fallback for local dev). Owner inbox at `/feedback`: New/Done/All tabs, mark-done/reopen. Live-verified end-to-end with SITE_PASSWORD set: featured list correct, POST open, GET/PATCH/inbox/owner-check 401 without password + working with it, built-in PUT/DELETE blocked for the public, /host + /designer publicly reachable, rate limit trips on the 6th post; screenshot-reviewed home/designer/inbox (which caught: public built-in cards showed Delete — now owner-only). XSS scanner extended to the 5 new screen files. Test count: **864** (+29).
- 2026-07-19: **PII scrub at the outbound AI boundary** — the last pre-August compliance code item, built to the second reviewer's design (which correctly killed the naive "full-name scrubber": arbitrary name detection can't work; known-roster removal can). New pure module `engine/pii-scrub.js`: roster names removed case-insensitively on word boundaries (conditional boundaries so names like "A+" match; longest-first so "Mary Jo" beats "Mary"; over-redaction accepted — it degrades AI output slightly, never classroom display) + email/phone/URL pattern redaction. Applied inside `AIService` real paths to COPIES only — process() responses AND instructions (resolved {{tokens}} embed student text), and generateFakeResponses' style examples; every game-time call site (ai-process, ai-eliminate, foreach aiInject, legacy close-submissions) passes `rosterNames`. The classroom's own data is never mutated. Verified by a boundary test that stubs `_callClaude` and asserts nothing name/email/phone-shaped leaves the server (and that the original response object is untouched), 11 pure-module tests, and a real-API weekend-poem playthrough. Deliberately separate from content-filter.js — display gating and subprocessor minimization are different concerns. Test count: **835** (+13).
- 2026-07-19: **Journal lockdown + Save-as-Recipe durability** (both from the second independent review). (1) `GET /api/rooms/:code/journal` — public until now, with journal entries carrying student names and hide/kick events, reachable by anyone with the wall-projected room code — requires the room's teacher PIN (`?pin=`) or the site password via basic auth; live-verified 403/403/200. (2) **"Save as Recipe" survived only until the next deploy**: user recipes lived solely in `recipes/user/` and Render resets the filesystem on every push — a doc-cross-referencing catch (db.js had `user_games` but no recipes table). New `user_recipes` Neon table mirrors the games pattern (save/insert-if-absent/delete/list); `recipe-loader.loadAllRecipes` accepts injected DB recipe objects through the same validate + compatibility pipeline as files (DB wins over same-id files); startup migrates filesystem strays; local no-DB dev unchanged. Live-proven: recipe saved via API with NO file written, server restarted, fresh process loaded it from Neon (14 → 15 recipes) and delete round-tripped. +4 loader tests. Test count: **822**. Also corrected the built-in recipe count (14, not 13).
- 2026-07-19: **P0: XSS sweep — untrusted text can no longer execute on any screen** (found by the second independent review from documents alone; code-confirmed). Real holes: student name+text hit `innerHTML` UNESCAPED on the host preview responses list (one of two branches — the drawing branch escaped, the text branch did not), the reveal `renderResponses` list, the relay shared-result on BOTH host and player, and the turn scoreboard (custom team names); teacher/AI-generated config leaked through rate-scale labels (`${s.label}` in the bar chart + pie titles, host and player), estimate answer/unit, and the editor's foreach preview; AI output rendered raw in the designer's "beyond what the framework can do" panel. A relay answer of `<img src=x onerror=...>` executed on the projector and every student device. Fixes: all sinks escaped; host `escapeHtml` upgraded to cover quotes (attribute contexts); player.js and designer.js gained escapers (had none). Already-safe by construction: `applyTemplate` (textContent), reveal/announce content, vote options, reveal-one, checklist rows, the AI review panel. **Enforcement:** `tests/screens/xss-sinks.test.js` — a statement-level scanner (multi-line aware) over all seven screen files that fails the suite on any HTML-building statement with unescaped interpolation; escape-internally helpers recognized; audited-numeric statements allowlisted with justifications; includes a self-test so the scanner can't rot silently. New CLAUDE.md rule: student text, teacher config, and AI output are all untrusted for rendering. Test count: **818** (+8).
- 2026-07-19: **P0 fixes from the independent review — flow-control gating + log scrubbing** — (1) **Flow control is teacher-only now**: the review caught `start-game`/`close-submissions`/`close-voting`/`advance-phase`/`relay-finish-all` accepting emits from ANY socket ("middle schoolers find this"); a full audit found 14 ungated flow events (also retry/skip-phase, reveal-next, close-ranking/-matching/-rating/-wager, wager-resolve, end-game). All now check `isTeacherSocket`; player gameplay events (submits, taps, describer actions) stay open by design, and turn got-it/skip were confirmed to validate the describer internally. Console sim grew intruder-can't-start and intruder-can't-close/advance invariants; full chaos suite + preview/reveal-one playthrough green. (2) **Student content never reaches production logs**: new `engine/content-log.js` (`contentLog` no-ops unless `DEBUG_CONTENT=1`) gates AI instructions/inputs/outputs and head-to-head vote winners (student answer text); join/submit/disconnect/eliminate/winner logs now carry socket ids + counts instead of names — Render's log retention no longer stores student data beyond our own 6h TTL story. Both COMPLIANCE-TODO P0s checked off. 810 tests green.
- 2026-07-19: **COPPA/FERPA hardening — AI data minimization + PIN lockout** — the two "before any real classroom use" code items from the California student-data review. (1) **Student names never reach the Claude API**: `AIService._buildUserMessage` and ai-eliminate's playerList now send pseudonymous playerIds + answer text only; new `engine/ai-name-fill.js` deep-fills the REAL names into AI JSON output afterward (any object with a recognized `playerId` gets truthful `playerName`), so shipped games like feedback-coach-academy that render `{{_current.playerName}}` keep working — and a model-garbled name echo can no longer beat the registry spelling. Generator guidance updated so AI-built games follow the contract. Verified by unit tests (a name in the outbound message is a test failure) + a real-API weekend-poem playthrough. (2) **Teacher-PIN brute-force lockout**: `engine/pin-throttle.js` (pure, injected clock — adjudicateTap pattern): 5 wrong PINs in 10 min lock a room's console joins for 5 min, even for the correct PIN, keyed by room code so a fresh socket doesn't reset it; success clears; hourly sweep. simulate-teacher-console grew the invariant (attacker spam → correct-PIN-on-fresh-socket still bounced). Also recorded two architectural rules in CLAUDE.md: no student name in any outbound API payload; saved objects (configs/recipes) never contain student-generated content. Snapshot lifecycle needed no work — purge-on-end + 6h TTL already beat the advice's 24-48h ask. Remaining workstream (documents, console verifications, watch list incl. AB 1159) captured in NEXT-STEPS §1.5. Test count: **810** (+14).
- 2026-07-19: **"Activity" vocabulary — copy sweep** — Lanyard increasingly makes things that aren't games (checklist, critique, Connection Pack, consensus polls), and "game" was hiding them: a teacher needing a lab checklist would never click "Build a Game". User-facing copy now says **activity** — the word teachers already use in lesson plans — across home ("Host an Activity" / "Build an Activity", "Interactive activities for your class"), designer ("Activity Designer", "What do you want to DO with your class?", "My Activities", "Create Activity", "Activity Settings"), host ("Choose an activity", "Start!", "End Session", "That's a wrap!"), player ("Join In", "Waiting to start...", "That's a wrap — thanks for playing along!"), teacher console, prototype, and server socket/API messages. "Game" survives only where something genuinely is one (elimination-game examples, end-phase messages in game configs). Considered "interaction" (rejected: nobody's natural vocabulary — a designer's word, not a teacher's). Deliberately copy-level only: `games/`, `gameId`, `GameEngine`, event names, and API routes unchanged — zero functional churn, all 796 tests green, home + designer screenshot-verified. Follow-up candidates: validator messages still say `Game "x":` (test-string churn), recipe `kind: game|activity` tag for per-item verbs.
- 2026-07-19: **Checklist phase (28th type) — shared group to-do list with a live dashboard** — the first pure classroom-management phase: teacher writes a task list, the class splits into groups (any team-split method; omit `teamsFrom` for one list per student), every group works the same checklist on their devices while the projector shows per-group progress bars with a small celebration as each group finishes. Any member checks or un-checks any item (merge's shared-draft trust model); checks carry attribution ("✓ Alice") shown to the group and the teacher console, never the projector. Teacher console gets full per-group detail + tap-to-toggle on a group's behalf (snapshot on mid-phase join too). Close is a summary moment (like sort/match — host clicks Continue); output `results`/`resultsList` feed downstream templates ("Great work today! {{worktime.resultsList}}"). Zero AI calls, no scores. Pure rules TDD'd in `engine/phases/checklist-state.js` (12 tests); kind-guarded idempotent `closeChecklist` in the advance-phase routing; reconnect restores board/summary; robot playtest + universal sim + chaos all play it. Shipped WITH its recipe (`recipes/group-work.json`: tasks + group size + how-groups-form + optional work timer — `$if` drops the timer cleanly) so it's reachable from the idea-first front door on day one. New game: **Group Work Day** (`games/group-work-day/`), verified by universal sim, chaos, live player screenshot, and editor Simple-view screenshot. Bonus fix the screenshots surfaced: match/sort sections were missing from BOTH screens' section registries — they stayed visible under whatever phase came next (latent since July 6). Test count: **796** (+12).
- 2026-07-19: **Open team capacity — pre-existing classroom teams** — `capacity:"open"` on team-split removes choice mode's even-split spot caps. The gap it closes: a class with 7 real teams of 4 and 2 absences got capacities 4,4,4,4,4,3,3, so a present member of a "full" capped team was locked out of their own team. With open capacity, `state.capacities` is null — no bounce in `team-pick`, rosters carry `capacity/open: null`, and every screen hides the spot counts (player pick cards show just the team name, host choice view drops "N spots left", teacher-arrange shows `(n)` instead of `(n/cap)`); straggler auto-fill already handled null caps (emptiest team). Default stays `"even"` — caps are right when kids pick freely. Validator warns when open is set on random/balanced (ignored there); editor gets a "Team spots" select (choice mode only); Simple view reads "students join their own team (no size caps)"; AI generator taught the pattern. Third split added to `games/_team-modes` + 4 new sim invariants (`simulate-team-modes.js` now 18: no-caps rosters, five-on-one-team pile-on, 5/0 split, custom names) plus a live player screenshot. New pure tests for `resolveTeamPlan`/`buildTeamRosters` (capacities moved into the plan). Test count: **784** (+7).
- 2026-07-06: **Drawing input v1 — students draw instead of type** — `collect.inputType:"drawing"` swaps the text box for a drawing pad. The first deliberate break from "games are text-based": strokes, not images — drawings travel as `{strokes:[{points,color,width}]}` with 0-1 normalized coordinates, so one drawing renders on a phone pad, a projector gallery card, and a 160px moderation thumbnail, and reveals can ANIMATE the strokes replaying. Shared browser module `screens/shared/drawing.js` (pointer-events pad covering mouse/touch/stylus, 7-color palette, undo/clear that never remove inherited strokes, `Draw.scribble()` for bots); pure server validation `engine/drawing.js` (clamp coords/width/color, trim to caps — 400 strokes/6000 points — reject only empty/malformed; TDD, 13 tests). Composes with the existing engine: **reveal-one** becomes an animated gallery with ✏️-name captions; **rotateFrom** rotates the actual strokes (`byPlayerDrawing`/`assignedDrawing`) so a drawing source preloads onto the recipient's pad (continue-the-drawing) or displays read-only above a text box (caption mode); **preview** shows the teacher real thumbnails before the class sees anything; the **teacher console** moderation list renders thumbnails (hide/kick) since a "[drawing]" placeholder is unmoderatable. Safety framing: the content filter can't read pictures, so drawing games lean on attribution + preview + console thumbnails — both shipped games route through preview before the projector. AI steps can't read drawings (validator warns on wiring one into ai-process). Editor: "Students answer with" toggle + Simple-view "Students draw:"; AI generator taught the pattern. Two games: **Art Gallery** (draw → preview → animated gallery) and **Finish My Drawing** (draw → pass-and-continue ×2 → preview → gallery — every picture has three artists). Verified by universal sim (which this work also fixed: it listened for 'preview' instead of 'preview-content' and had NO reveal-one support — every preview/reveal-one game stalled silently), chaos on both games, and live screenshots (which caught the recurring display-vs-`[hidden]` CSS bug on the caption-preview canvas). v2 (live mural / tile-wall phase) deliberately deferred until the widget and safety patterns prove out. Test count: **777** (+13).
- 2026-07-06: **Sort phase (27th type) — place items into named buckets** — categorization as a first-class step (metaphor vs simile, fact vs opinion). Deliberate UI call: tap-to-assign (each item shows the buckets as toggle buttons; Submit unlocks when everything's placed) instead of the originally-imagined drag — dragging items across 2-4 bucket zones is miserable on a phone, and the cognition is identical. Two modes decided by the items themselves (estimate's poll-mode precedent): every item carries a correct bucket → graded (`pointsPerItem` each, scoreMap output for leaderboards); none do → consensus poll (distributions only). Mixed configs are a validation error. Close is the discussion moment: per-item class distributions with the correct bucket highlighted green + accuracy % on the projector; personal score + answer key (and perfect-sort confetti) on player devices; host clicks Continue. Items shuffled server-side (Fisher-Yates); timer auto-submits partial boards; pure scoring TDD'd in `engine/phases/sort-scoring.js` (16 tests); kind-guarded idempotent `closeSorting` in the advance-phase routing; reconnect restores board/waiting/results. Editor: buckets row-list + item rows with a correct-bucket dropdown ("no right answer" = consensus), Simple-view sentence, token/data-ref pickers, valid-out-of-the-box default step; AI generator taught the pattern. Simulator bots (robot playtest + chaos + universal sim) all play it. New game: **Metaphor or Simile?** (`games/metaphor-or-simile/` — graded round + Genius-vs-Chaos consensus round → leaderboard), verified by sim playthrough, chaos, and live board/results screenshots. Test count: **764** (+16).
- 2026-07-06: **Teams upgrade — group size, teacher-arranged, and student-picked teams** — team-split now sizes by `teamCount` OR `groupSize` (exactly one; `engine/phases/team-grouping.js` computes the count — 22 kids in groups of 4 → 4,4,4,4,3,3, an odd pair-class forms a triple, never a singleton). Two new interactive methods join random/balanced: **teacher** (host screen shows unassigned name chips + team cards — tap a name, tap a team, tap an assigned name to send it back; Confirm auto-fills the rest) and **choice** (students tap the group they want on their device — open spots only, full teams bounce just the tapper, switching allowed until close; all-placed auto-closes, Confirm sweeps stragglers into the emptiest teams). Both end in the standard TEAM_SPLIT reveal with the unchanged `{teams, playerTeam}` output, so turn/relay/leaderboard consume every method identically; random mode also swapped its biased sort-shuffle for Fisher-Yates. Privileged events (`team-assign`, `team-split-confirm`) require `isTeacherSocket`; a player emitting them is ignored (sim-verified). New pure module TDD'd (12 tests); interactive state is kind-guarded phaseState with an idempotent `closeTeamSplit` wired into advance-phase routing; reconnect restores the pick screen mid-choice. Verified by `scripts/simulate-team-modes.js` (14 invariants: capacities, full-team bounce, straggler auto-fill, assign/unassign round-trip, explicit-assignment survival, intruder rejection) against the hidden `games/_team-modes` fixture, plus universal-sim and chaos runs (and a charades-bowl chaos regression for random mode). Editor: method dropdown grew the two modes, "Size teams by" toggle swaps teamCount/groupSize inputs, Simple view reads "The class splits into groups of 4 — students pick their own spots." Test count: **748** (+14).
- 2026-07-06: **Match phase (26th type) — pair two lists, auto-scored** — students see the left column fixed and drag the right column into alignment (drag-to-SWAP, not splice-reorder, so fixing one row never breaks another; touch + mouse + arrow-button fallback). Config: `pairs` (literal `{left, right}` array, ≥2, unique texts both sides, warn >8), `pointsPerMatch` (default 10), timer auto-submits the current board. Close is an estimate-style discussion moment: correct pairs + per-pair class accuracy bars on the projector, personal "you matched X of Y" + full answer key on player devices (confetti on a perfect board); host clicks Continue. Output `scores` is a scoreMap — leaderboards sum it like any graded phase. Pure scoring in `engine/phases/match-scoring.js` (TDD, 14 tests); kind-guarded idempotent `closeMatching` wired into the advance-phase routing; reconnect restores board/waiting/results; right column dealt with Fisher-Yates + re-deal if it lands solved (a pre-solved board actually happened in testing). Simulator bots (robot playtest + chaos + universal sim) all play it; editor gets a pairs row widget, Simple-view sentence with inline pair editing, token/data-ref picker entries, and a works-out-of-the-box default step. New game: **Vocab Match** (`games/vocab-match/` — French vocab + inventors rounds → leaderboard), verified by full sim playthrough, chaos suite, and live screenshots. Two screenshot-caught fixes: the dealt-solved board, and `button { display:block }` overriding `[hidden]` (the recurring CSS bug — added `button[hidden]` override). Test count: **734** (+14).

- 2026-05-19: **Safety increment 2 — host moderation** — `engine/moderation.js` (pure helpers: `responseToText`, `hasSubmitted`, `isVisibleSubmission`, `buildSubmissionList`). New events `SUBMISSIONS_UPDATE` / `MODERATE_HIDE` / `MODERATE_KICK` / `KICKED`. Server: `emitSubmissionsUpdate()` pushes the live submission list (name + text + hidden) to the host on each submit; hide toggles `player.responseHidden` (excluded from AI input + reveal at close-submissions, reversible); kick removes the player, blocks rejoin via per-room `kickedTokens`, and notifies the kicked socket. Host UI: collapsible "🛡 Moderation (N)" panel on the collect screen with Hide/Unhide + Kick per row, plus a ✕ kick button in the lobby player list. Player: `kicked` handler clears identity (keeps token so the block sticks); manual join now resends the saved token (per-room, so harmless cross-room). Tests: `moderation.test.js`. Test count: **475** (+10).
- 2026-05-19: **Safety increment 1 — input safety pipeline** — `engine/content-filter.js` + `engine/blocklist.js`. `filterContent()` word-boundary blocklist match with leet-speak / repeated-letter / separator normalization (no Scunthorpe false positives); `validateResponse()` length/mash/prompt-copy; `checkSubmission()` gates string + multi-field object responses. Wired into `submit-response` (free-text collect only; rejects → `RESPONSE_REJECTED`, nothing stored). Player UX: char counter, 280 maxlength, min-length nudge, red rejection notice that preserves text. AI: `SAFETY_RULES` block (content-safety + anti-prompt-injection) appended to every game-run system prompt in `_processReal`. Tests: `content-filter.test.js`. Test count: **465** (+21). Scoped from `SAFETY-DESIGN.md`; remaining increments: anonymous mode, rate limiting / DoS limits, PII redaction, relay-text filtering.
- 2026-05-19: **YouTube video embed** — `video: "<youtube url>"` field on announce/reveal/collect/collect-choice (same 4 phases as `image`). `engine/video.js#resolveVideoEmbed()` parses watch/youtu.be/embed/shorts URLs (+ `t=`/`start=` offsets) into an embed URL; returns null for non-YouTube input. Host-only by design — renders in an `<iframe class="phase-video">` on the projector screen (added to each phase's `hostToggles` as `'video'`, NOT playerToggles). `showSection()` blanks all iframe srcs on transition so audio never bleeds across phases. Editor: plain URL text field after the image widget. AI generator/recipe matcher pick it up automatically (schema-driven prompt). Tests: `video-embed.test.js` (parser) + `video-field.test.js` (validator allow-list). Test count: **444** (+17).

- 2026-05-03: Per-player AI primitive — `ai-process` accepts `perPlayer: true` (engine generates N items, stores `byPlayer:{pid:item}`); new `{{X.mine}}` template token resolves per-recipient in collect/collect-choice prompts, announce, and reveal. Host sees `(each student gets their own)` placeholder.
- 2026-05-03: Synthetic `.list` resolver — `{{X.list}}` renders an array as a numbered text list (1. item / 2. item / ...). Added to AI generator prompt as the right way to display arrays.
- 2026-05-03: Save endpoint auto-strips unknown fields before validation (mirror of AI generator strip pass). Stale invented fields no longer block save.
- 2026-05-03: Foreach pattern — added "Guess the right answer" alongside spot-the-lie. `_current.shuffledFields` now excludes prompt-style keys (question/prompt/scenario/topic).
- 2026-05-03: Simple Poll template now uses collect-choice + barChart to match its bar-graph icon.
- 2026-05-03: collect/collect-choice prompts now resolve `{{...}}` refs (latent bug — they were being passed through unresolved).
- 2026-05-03: collect-choice responses store both `choice` and `text` so AI summarize sees the answer.
- 2026-05-03: Ask AI modal — fixed `display:flex` overriding the `[hidden]` attribute (modal popped open every page load and ignored close button).
- 2026-05-03: Prototype Skip Timer button — clicks whichever advance/close button is currently visible on host via postMessage, bypassing iframe focus issues.
- 2026-05-03: **Server crash fix** — stale `setTimeout` auto-advances in announce/eliminate/ai-eliminate/leaderboard/winner now check `ctx.isStale()` before transitioning. Skipping past a timed phase used to leave a leftover timer that fired later and threw an invalid-transition exception, killing the process.
- 2026-05-03: **Synthetic suffix resolver generalized** — `.list`/`.barChart`/`.pieChart`/`.chart` now work at any nesting depth. `{{collect-id.responses.list}}` renders the responses array as a numbered list. Bare `{{X.barChart}}` still reads `X.tally` (backward compat). New `formatList` helper auto-pulls arrays from `.result`/`.responses`/`.standings`.
- 2026-05-03: **Validator: cycle detection** — DFS over `next`/`approveNext` (excluding `loopBack` and `rejectNext`, which are intentional back-edges) flags any unintended loop as an error. Caught a real bug in corn-story (stub `phase-16` looping back into round1).
- 2026-05-03: **Validator: raw-array template warning** — scans `template`/`content`/`message`/`prompt`/`instruction`/`hostTemplate`/`playerTemplate` for `{{X.field}}` where field is a known-array (`responses`, `standings`, `eliminated`, `survivors`, `winnerIds`/`Names`, `rankings`, `matchups`, `candidateIds`, `voters`) without a `.list`/chart suffix. Warns to add `.list`. Caught 3 real bugs in feedback-coach-academy on first run.
- 2026-05-03: **Validator: design-hole warnings** — `scanForDesignHoles()` warns on (a) `wager` with no `scoresFrom` and no `correctOption` (host has to pick winner manually, players bet on nothing meaningful), (b) `team-split` with no downstream template referencing team data (wasted setup).
- 2026-05-03: **Wager default starting pool** — handler now gives every eligible player 100 points when `scoresFrom` isn't configured. Simplest case (one-off bet, no prior score chain) just works.
- 2026-05-03: **Corn Story rewrite** — deleted broken stub `phase-16`, repointed `crown.next → end`, fixed lobby/intro/collect ordering (intro now precedes collect), added 3 "Here's what everyone said" reveals using `{{X.responses.list}}`. 19 phases (was 16). Test asserting count updated.
- 2026-05-03: **Dream Vacation Debate rewrite** — dropped unused `team-split` and `wager` (no points basis, no objective winner — teams collaborated on one shared pitch, so "which team won?" had no answer). Flow now: lobby → submit → show-suggestions → rank → announce-winner (full ranking) → relay → pitch-reveal → end. Bumped announce timer 8s → 20s. Relay prompt now references `{{rank-destinations.rankings.0.item}}` so players have inline context.
- 2026-05-03: **Relay phase fixes**:
  - Player UI: added `#relay-prompt-display` element. Players now see the prompt above the running text on both their turn and while waiting.
  - Relay handler now resolves `{{...}}` refs in prompt via `ctx.resolveTemplate()` (latent bug — prompt was passed through raw).
  - Server emits `prompt` in `relay-waiting` event (was only sent on `relay-turn`).
  - New `relay-finish-all` socket event — host can fast-forward all remaining turns. Each remaining player gets `(skipped)`, then phase advances.
  - Host's `prototype-skip` handler detects when `relay-section` is visible and emits `relay-finish-all` (relay has no host-side button).
  - Prototype Bot Fill now repeats 12× over ~7s so each rotating active player gets filled in sequence.
- 2026-05-03: **Bot-fill regression fix** — earlier same-day change made bot-fill loop unconditionally for ~7s. That auto-skipped the next phase (e.g. vote screen never visible — players auto-voted before the screen rendered). Loop now only continues while the host iframe's `relay-section` is visible; non-relay phases get a single shot. Cap raised to 25 shots (~15s) for the relay path. Same-origin DOM read used to check host state; cross-origin fallback is single-shot.
- 2026-05-03: **Validator: unreachable-phase detection** — BFS from lobby across `next`/`approveNext`/`rejectNext`/`loopBack`. Any orphan phase warns (e.g. `winner` with no `next` strands `end`). Editor had this client-side but server didn't — now it does, so saved-via-API configs can't slip through. `detectUnreachablePhases()` in `engine/game-loader.js`.
- 2026-05-03: **Last One Standing rewrite** (`games/elimination-game/config.json`) — was 1 round, called itself "Last One Standing" but crowned a winner immediately. Now: 3-round loop on `eliminate.loopBack: round-intro, loopCount: 3`. Added `show-answers` reveal between collect and vote. `voting.voters: "remaining"` so eliminated players don't vote in later rounds. Added `winner.next: "end"` (caught by the new unreachable check). Round intro shows `Round X of Y` via `{{_loop.eliminate.iteration}}`.
- 2026-05-03: **ARCHITECTURE.md rewrite** (`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`) — replaced the stale doc (claimed 2,376-line server.js, 900-line handlePhase, 264 tests, separate voteState/rankState properties — none current). Now reflects: registry-based phase handlers (`engine/phase-handlers/`), `phase-context.js` API, `room.phaseState` single bag with auto-cleanup, `phaseInstanceId` staleness checks, journal ring buffer, paused-error recovery, validator pipeline (errors + warnings), 281 tests, full editor→save→run lifecycle, current REST endpoints (7 AI-related + CRUD).
- 2026-05-05: **Phase D — AI generator reads from schema** (`services/ai-service.js`) — new `buildPhaseDocsForPrompt({format})` derives field listings for both LIGHT_REVIEW_PROMPT (terse one-line per type) and GAME_GENERATOR_PROMPT (verbose with field types) directly from `PHASE_SCHEMAS`. Hand-curated prose preserved via `PHASE_EXTRA_GUIDANCE` map (multi-field collect, per-player AI, foreach guidance). Adding a new phase to the schema now flows automatically into the AI prompts.
- 2026-05-05: **Phase E — resolver grammar + typed dataflow** (`engine/resolver-grammar.js`, new) — single source of truth for `{{...}}` syntax. Four exports: `parseTemplateTokens` (find tokens in a string), `parseRef` (structural parse: kind/segments/suffix), `classifyRef` (schema-aware: producedType/capability/renderable/canonicalForm/problem), `checkDataRefCompat` (does ref satisfy `accepts: [{type, capability}]`?). Validator and engine both run refs through this module so they can't drift on what's a valid token. `KNOWN_SUFFIXES = {list, count, json, barChart, pieChart, chart, mine}`. `BUILTIN_SCOPES = {remaining, eliminated, players, _current, _foreach, _candidates, _loop}`.
- 2026-05-05: **Validator — schema-driven raw-array scan** — `scanTemplatesForRawArrays` rewritten to use `parseTemplateTokens` + `classifyRef`. Replaces hardcoded `KNOWN_ARRAY_FIELDS` set; typing now flows from `phase-schemas.js` outputs. Adding a new array output to a schema participates automatically.
- 2026-05-05: **Validator — typed-dataflow scan** — new `scanDataRefTypeMismatches` walks every `dataRef` field on every phase, looks up the producer's output type, and emits `DATA_REF_TYPE_MISMATCH` warnings via `checkDataRefCompat`. Surfaces silent type drift (e.g. wiring a leaderboard to `ai-process.result` when `format: json` returns an object — would render as empty leaderboard at runtime). Caught real bugs in feedback-academy, feedback-coach-academy, mad-lib-mashup on first run.
- 2026-05-05: **Engine resolver uses parseRef** — `engine/game-engine.js#resolve()` now dispatches via `parseRef` rather than its own ad-hoc string split. Validator and engine share one grammar; behavior identical (47 game-engine tests still pass). Renderable suffixes (`list/barChart/pieChart/chart`) handled in engine; `mine` is rewritten by server-side per-player helper before resolve runs; `count/json` are validator-only annotations.
- 2026-05-05: **Schema fix — vote/rank candidates accept responseArray** — `vote.candidates` and `rank.candidates` were declared `accepts: [candidateSource]` only, but voting/ranking on raw collected responses is a primary use case. Now both also accept `responseArray`. Removed false-positive DATA_REF_TYPE_MISMATCH warnings on corn-story and dream-vacation.
- 2026-05-05: **Test count: 298** (`npm test`). New diagnostic-codes covered: `DATA_REF_TYPE_MISMATCH` fixture in `tests/engine/validator-diagnostics.test.js`. Snapshots updated for feedback-academy, feedback-coach-academy, mad-lib-mashup to reflect newly-surfaced bugs.
- 2026-05-06: **Recipe layer R1 (foundation)** — `engine/recipe-schema.js` (Recipe shape, validateRecipe, validateParams returning structured Diagnostics), `engine/recipe-compiler.js` (coerce form-strings → declared types, apply defaults, substitute `${param}` placeholders with whole-value type preservation), `engine/recipe-loader.js` (scans `recipes/` at startup, caches in memory, separate `recipes/user/` path for R5). First recipe `recipes/class-poll.json`. New endpoints: `GET /api/recipes`, `GET /api/recipes/:id`, `POST /api/recipes/:id/compile`. Compiled configs run through `validate()` as a safety net. Test count: **368** (+70). Plan in `docs/RECIPE-LAYER.md`.
- 2026-05-06: **Recipe layer R2 (picker UI)** — Designer landing page now has a primary "⭐ Use a Recipe" button. `screens/designer/designer.js` adds `showRecipePicker()`, parameter-form auto-renderer (one widget per type: text/textarea/number/checkbox/select/array-repeater), submit flow (gather → compile → save → redirect to editor). Inline error display reads structured Diagnostics. Form-string coercion handled server-side by the compiler ("30" → 30 for integer fields). CSS in `screens/designer/styles.css`. The phase-graph editor stays as the post-creation editing surface.
- 2026-05-06: **Recipe layer R3 (seed library)** — Five new hand-built recipes shipped: `discussion-starter` (collect → AI summarize themes → reveal), `creative-vote` (collect → vote pick-one → winner), `elimination-tournament` (round-intro → collect → vote → eliminate bottom-% → loop → winner; uses `loopBack`/`loopCount` driven by recipe param), `anonymous-feedback` (intro about anonymity → collect → AI paraphrase themes → reveal), `story-builder` (relay turns → reveal `{{story.text}}`). All recipes auto-validated by `tests/engine/recipes-integration.test.js` — every recipe must compile cleanly with sample params and produce a config that passes `validate()`. **6 recipes total** in the picker. `quiz-show` deferred — needs compiler extension for array-driven phase generation; documented in `docs/RECIPE-LAYER.md` as future R3.5.
- 2026-05-06: **Recipe layer R4 (AI as recipe matcher)** — `aiService.matchRecipe(description, recipes)` calls Haiku with a system prompt listing every available recipe + its parameters. Output is a tiny structured object: `{recipe: 'id', params: {...}}` or `{noMatch: true, reason, suggestion}`. New endpoint `POST /api/games/from-description`. Designer's "AI Generate Game" now uses this flow: description → AI matches recipe → preview the recipe + filled params → confirm → save + redirect. The legacy whole-config Sonnet generator is preserved as `showLegacyAIGenerateModal` (renamed) and reachable from the no-match view as "Generate Custom (Advanced)". **This resolves the JSON malformation bug class** — AI emits ~5 fields, not 200 lines, so parse errors at character 14640 stop happening. Cost: ~10× cheaper per generation (Haiku, 800 max-tokens vs Sonnet, 4096 max-tokens + auto-polish review).
- 2026-05-07: **Recipe layer R5 (save-as-recipe)** — `engine/recipe-extractor.js` adds `extractCandidates(config)` (walks every phase, returns all parameterizable fields with auto-suggested names + labels; collision-aware naming) and `buildUserRecipe(config, paramSpecs, metadata)` (substitutes `${name}` placeholders, preserves enum.values/array.item/integer.min-max/string.minLength-maxLength from the source phase schema, validates). New endpoints `POST /api/recipes/draft` (returns candidates) and `POST /api/recipes/user` (builds + smoke-tests + saves to `recipes/user/{id}.json`, busts cache). Editor toolbar adds "⭐ Save as Recipe" button → 2-step modal (candidate checklist with editable name+label per row, then recipe metadata). Round-trip property tested: game → extract → build → compile produces the same game back. Built-in id collisions blocked. Test count: **389** (+21).
- 2026-05-07: **Recipe layer R6 (polish)** — compatibility check in `engine/recipe-loader.js` compiles each loaded recipe with synthetic defaults + runs the result through `validate()`; on failure, flags `_broken: true` + `_brokenReason` instead of silently dropping. `summarizeRecipe()` now exposes `source: 'built-in'|'user'`, `broken`, `brokenReason` so the picker can render warning badges + group sections. New `DELETE /api/recipes/user/:id` endpoint (refuses built-ins, busts cache). Designer picker now renders "My Recipes" + "Built-in Recipes" sections, with delete buttons on user cards and orange "⚠ Needs update" badges on broken cards (broken recipes sort to the bottom of their section). Test count: **392** (+3). **Recipe layer plan R1–R6 complete.**

- 2026-05-12: **Rotation primitive (SCAMPER)** — `collect.rotateFrom: "<phaseId>"` makes the engine assign each eligible player a different player's item from the named source phase. `{{<phaseId>.assigned}}` token renders that item per-recipient (resolved by `resolvePerPlayerTemplate`). The assignment map is stored on the SOURCE phase (`engine.phaseData[source].assigned`) so a downstream collect can chain by simply referencing the previous collect by id. Cyclic shift `((i - offset) % N + N) % N` guarantees no self-assignment and full coverage. New game `games/scamper/` (19 phases). Validator + grammar both extended with `.assigned` suffix. Schema-driven so the editor sidebar can expose it later. End-to-end verified by `scripts/simulate-scamper.js`. Test count: **405**.
- 2026-05-12: **Rate phase** — `engine/phase-handlers/rate.js`, schema entry, server `closeRating` helper, host + player UI. Phase takes a `scales` array of `{id, label, min, max, labels?}` objects and a `visibility: "all"|"host-only"` flag. Players see N button rows (one per scale), submit all at once. Server aggregates averages + per-scale distribution and emits `RATE_RESULTS`. Critically, the handler does NOT auto-advance — the host clicks Continue (advance-phase) so everyone has time to read the chart. Player must be brought back to rate-section on results event (they were pushed to waiting on submit). Results rendered as a horizontal averages bar chart (red→green hue interpolated from value/min/max) + per-scale conic-gradient pie charts with legend. First rate game: `games/class-critique/`.
- 2026-05-12: **Phase image uploads** — `image: "assets/foo.jpg"` field on announce/reveal/collect/collect-choice. New endpoint `POST /api/games/:gameId/assets` (multer, 5MB cap, JPEG/PNG/GIF/WebP only) sanitizes filenames and writes to `games/<id>/assets/`. Express now serves `/games/<id>/...` statically. Server-side `resolveImageUrl()` builds the runtime web path from the config-relative path. Editor sidebar widget on those 4 phase types: file input + thumbnail + remove + drag-and-drop zone with hover highlight. Host/player visibility controlled via the existing `hostShow`/`playerShow` toggles (`'image'` added to each phase's toggle vocab).
- 2026-05-12: **Auto-save + click-outside-collapse** — `deselectPhase` now awaits an `autoSaveIfDirty()` PUT before clearing the selection. A document-level click listener detects clicks outside `.phase-box` (and outside modals / validation / review panels) and triggers deselect. Saves silently; surfaces validation errors via the existing panel (and keeps the form open so user can fix). Brand-new games (no `gameId`) skip auto-save until first manual Save to avoid the ID prompt firing on every click-off.
- 2026-05-12: **Inline-form click bug fix** — clicks on text inputs in the inline-attached phase config bubbled to the phase-box's click handler, which called `selectPhase` → `renderPhaseConfig` and destroyed focus mid-edit. `handlePhaseClick` now short-circuits if the click target is inside `.phase-box-inline-form`. Number inputs masked the bug because the browser handled keyboard arrow input on the re-rendered focused field; text inputs lost characters entirely.
- 2026-05-12: **Designer-vibe default theme** — host + player JS now apply the `pop-art` theme preset on load (before socket connection), so even the lobby / room-code screens have the Keith Haring vibe (cream canvas, black borders, flat colors). Base `body` font bumped to `Arial Black` + 3px black-bordered inputs/buttons/prompts on both host and player CSS so the styling reads as bold even when no theme overrides apply. Game-specified themes still win — they're applied after on `room-created`.
- 2026-05-12: **Idea Chain recipe** — `recipes/idea-chain.json`. 4-round telephone using `rotateFrom`. Params: starter prompt, transform prompt (reused all 4 rounds), timer. Phase shape: lobby → intro → starter collect → 4× (announce + collect rotateFrom previous round) → reveal starters → reveal finals → end. Hand-coded 4 rounds because the compiler doesn't yet support array-driven phase generation (deferred along with quiz-show).
- 2026-05-12: **Class Critique recipe** — `recipes/class-critique.json`. Uses the rate phase. Params: topic, three scale labels (defaults Originality/Effectiveness/Feasibility — interpolated into the `scales` array via `${scale1Label}` string substitution), visibility, timer. All scales are 1-5 with no end labels; teachers can edit further in the editor afterward.
- 2026-05-12: **AI generator awareness of rotation + rate** — added `PHASE_EXTRA_GUIDANCE.rate` and a rotation block under `PHASE_EXTRA_GUIDANCE.collect` in `services/ai-service.js`. Includes concrete examples (chain of rotateFrom collects, rate scales array shape). The recipe matcher picks up new recipes automatically since recipes are passed in as the source of truth — confirmed live: "telephone game where each person sees the previous answer" matches `idea-chain`; "students rate a presentation on originality, clarity and impact" matches `class-critique` with the custom scale labels auto-extracted.
- 2026-05-12: **Render-ready** — `PORT = Number(process.env.PORT) || 3000` so Render's assigned port is honored. `package.json` gets `engines: {node: ">=18.0.0"}`. `.gitignore` extended for `recipes/user/`, `.env.*.local`, common OS/editor noise. Static-serve mount for `/games/<id>/...` already in place from the image upload work, which doubles as the runtime path for any per-game asset.
- 2026-05-12: **Test count: 427** (`npm test`). New test files: `rotate-from.test.js`, `rate-phase.test.js`, `image-field.test.js`. Snapshots auto-updated for the two new games (`scamper`, `class-critique` — both clean, empty diagnostic sets).

- 2026-05-13 → 2026-06-08 (condensed — this period went unlogged; see `git log` for detail):
  - **Bluffing primitives** — `collect.assign:"pairwise"`, `vote.matchupsFromPairs`/`excludeAuthors`, `collect-choice.choicePool`/`excludeAuthored`/`shuffle`. Games: Quiplash-style, Fibbage, Definition Bluff, Yes-or-No Bets (later renamed to avoid trademark conflicts).
  - **Speed-bonus scoring** — `collect-choice` with `correctAnswer` + `speedBonus`; Kahoot-style time-decay points in pure `engine/speed-scoring.js`. Game: Speed Quiz.
  - **Turn primitive + Charades Bowl** — Fishbowl-style describe/act/one-word rounds; server-authoritative per-turn timer, team rotation, Got It/Skip pool.
  - **Safety pipeline** — `engine/content-filter.js` + blocklist (leet-speak normalization) gating `submit-response`; AI safety-rules block on every game-run system prompt; host moderation panel (hide/kick) on collect phases.
  - **YouTube embeds** — `video:` field on announce/reveal/collect/collect-choice (host-only); `engine/video.js` URL parser.
  - **Neon Postgres persistence** — user-created games in `user_games` table (`db.js`); survives Render redeploys. Filesystem fallback when `DATABASE_URL` unset.
  - **Password gate** — `SITE_PASSWORD` env var → HTTP Basic Auth on /host, /designer, /prototype. Student paths stay open.
  - **Designer humanization series** — primary field inline on phase boxes, template tokens shown as friendly labels with live preview, "+ Insert from earlier step" picker, data-refs named by the actual question text, settings panel declutter, game categories.
  - **Rebrand: Classroom Games → Lanyard** — plus home screen redesign (two primary cards + student room-code join), STYLE-GUIDE.md, site-wide style alignment, emoji cleanup.
  - **Rank drag-and-drop** — HTML5 drag + touch; arrow buttons kept as fallback. Plus comma-separated string candidates accepted (AI generators emit that format).
  - **Prototype carousel view** — "one at a time" player navigation alongside Grid.
  - **Misc** — leaderboard ties share medals (Olympic ranking), `ai-process perPlayer` auto-retry on JSON parse failure, AI endpoints fail loudly when no API key, render.yaml.

- 2026-06-09: **Connection Pack** (commit 481dd4c) — first no-winner game family (spec: `docs/connection-pack-spec.md`). Three experiences as recipes + compiled games: **Closer** (escalating pair conversations, ~90 original prompts in 3 tiers), **Snowball** (think-pair-share), **One Voice** (cooperative counting). Two new phase types (22 + 23): **merge** (shared live draft, last-write-wins, `agreeMode` both/any/timer, quad-join) and **one-voice** (server-authoritative tap adjudication via pure `adjudicateTap` with injected clock; teacher-speaker Web Speech audio). Shared primitives: `passAllowed` (pass is never attributable anywhere), `simultaneousReveal` (nameless ticker), `reveal.scope:"pair"` + `{{_pair.*}}`, `family:"connection"` (validator permanently rejects winners/elimination/graded scoring), recipe `feel` tags. Pairing engine: greedy non-repeat matching, `oddHandling:"triple"`, `rotatePairsFrom`/`reusePairsFrom`. Zero AI calls in default paths.
- 2026-06-09: **Idea-first front door** (e4d31a4) — `/designer` opens with one big box ("What do you want to play with your class?") routing through from-description recipe matching. Example chips, `?idea=` deep-link, old three buttons demoted to links.
- 2026-06-09: **Robot playtest in "Check for Errors"** (e3ddbed) — deep review now also PLAYS the game: `services/simulator.js` self-connects 1 host + 4 bots in a hidden temp room (mock AI, zero spend), parallel with the Sonnet review. Catches stalls, crashes, blank screens, empty payloads, unresolved `{{tokens}}`, `[object Object]`. First run caught a real shipped bug (mood-check's `{{process.result.content}}`).
- 2026-06-09: **Teacher console** (a889418) — `/teacher` private second-device view; the host screen is PROJECTED, so "teacher-only" UI there was actually public. Room code + 4-digit PIN (click-to-reveal chip on host screen) or SITE_PASSWORD basic-auth. Live moderation with names, preview approve/reject, close/next controls. Privileged socket actions check `isTeacherSocket`.

- 2026-06-09: **Prototype carousel polish** (4 commits) — dots centered over the player panel with a dark pill; arrows flank the player panel only; chrome appears only in one-at-a-time mode and only after Launch.
- 2026-06-09: **Prompt-aware Bot Fill** (3f28610) — `screens/shared/bot-brain.js`: pure rules, no AI. Keyword banks, embedded-choice picking, yes/no detection, multi-field placeholder matching.

- 2026-06-09: **Simple view — the plain-English editor** (90b0b83 + 3 fix commits) — editor now DEFAULTS to a sentence-per-step view with inline editable boxes; phase canvas demoted to "Advanced". Polished via self-screenshot review (new workflow: launch server → headless screenshots → review own renders) and a 9-game sweep; "Advanced settings" deep-links to the expanded step.
- 2026-06-09: **Teacher console screenshot review + tooling** (5ea04e9) — header wrap fixed, count seeds on join, "Next step" hidden during preview (couldn't-skip-review), empty controls bar hidden. New permanent tools: `scripts/screenshot.js` (CDP headless screenshots — socket pages hang plain `--virtual-time-budget`) and `scripts/demo-room.js` (holds a live room with bots, prints CODE/PIN).
- 2026-06-09: **Test count: 642** (`npm test`).
- 2026-06-10: **Juice pack** (closes the 2026-06 strategic review) — `screens/shared/juice.js`: Web Audio synthesized SFX (no asset files; cues `pop`/`blip`/`reveal`/`tada`/`fanfare`/`womp`/`tick`, per-cue 150ms throttle, lazy AudioContext unlocked on first gesture), self-cleaning canvas confetti (colors read from the active theme's CSS vars; honors `prefers-reduced-motion`), deterministic emoji avatars (`avatarFor(name)` — same animal on host lobby, leaderboard, winner standings, team cards, and the student's own device), persisted mute toggle (🔊 chip top-right on host). Theme-level config: each preset theme declares `juice.wave` (pop-art triangle, arcade square, ocean/sunset sine) via `window.__themeJuice`. Host cues: join pop, submission/vote/rank/rate/wager blips, reveal chimes, leaderboard tada + staggered row pop-in, elimination womp, winner fanfare + confetti, one-voice success confetti, final-5-seconds timer ticks. Player devices stay quiet except their own moments: submit blip + bounce, personal confetti for winners / rank-1 / one-voice success, womp if eliminated. Every entry point guarded — juice failure can never break gameplay. Verified via lobby screenshot harness (host + 4 players). Tests: `juice.test.js`. Test count: **650** (+8).
- 2026-06-10: **No more visible {{code}} anywhere a teacher reads** — three-layer fix after user spotted `{{ai-mashup.list}}` in Simple view. (1) Simple view: token-aware text boxes — content with `{{refs}}` renders as editable text segments with friendly chips ("list of answers from step 5", "the winning answer from step 8", × to remove, hover for raw token); config stays byte-exact. (2) Advanced view: the canvas primary textarea now tokenizes refs to `[Winning answer — step 8]` labels (same tokenize/detokenize round-trip the screen-control fields use); `buildTemplateVariables` vocabulary expanded from 4 ref kinds to the full resolver surface (winner/list/barChart/tally/mine/assigned/rankedList/merged.list/one-voice stats/eliminated.length/remaining.length) with **step-unique labels** — fixes a latent corruption bug where two same-type steps shared a label ("AI result") and detokenize silently rewired refs to the wrong step; insert-menu items now insert friendly labels; `phaseContentLabel` strips tokens so step references in sentences never show braces. (3) Game sweep: full token inventory across all configs surfaced feedback-coach-academy showing students literal `{{_current.critique}}` (reveal-one resolves `message` once — no item scope) and raw JSON items. New `itemTemplate` field on reveal-one (`formatRevealItem` pure + tested) renders each item via `{{_current.field}}`; new validator rule `SPECIAL_SCOPE_OUT_OF_CONTEXT` warns whenever `_current`/`_foreach`/`_candidates`/`_pair` appear where they can't resolve ("students will see the raw code on screen"). Repaired feedback-coach-academy (itemTemplate) and feedback-academy (stale fields, `[object Object]` scenario prompt → `result.work`/`result.context`). Test count: **658** (+8).
- 2026-06-10: **AI cost guards** — `services/ai-budget.js`: every real Anthropic call now passes through `AIService._callClaude` (single choke point; all 10 `messages.create` sites converted), gated by two env-configurable guards: sliding per-minute throttle (`AI_CALLS_PER_MINUTE`, default 20) and daily cap (`AI_DAILY_CAP`, default 500; 0 disables either). Day counter persists in Neon (`ai_usage` table) when `DATABASE_URL` is set, so a redeploy/restart can't reset the cap — verified live. Blocked calls throw `AiBudgetError` (statusCode 429, teacher-readable message naming the env var + retry-after seconds) BEFORE reaching the API, so they cost nothing. Failure modes: editor endpoints return HTTP 429 with the friendly message; in-game ai-process surfaces through the existing phase-error pause (Retry works after the window clears); fake-responses/theme fall back to mock; auto-polish skips. Mock mode never reaches the guard. `GET /api/ai-budget` shows today's usage (async `peek()` loads the persisted count so it's truthful right after restart). Closes the "no app-level spend protection" gap (Anthropic Console limit was the only backstop). Tests: `ai-budget.test.js` (injected clock — throttle window slide, daily rollover, store resume, broken-store tolerance). Test count: **669** (+11).
- 2026-06-10: **Recipe compiler conditionals (R7)** — closes the limitation hit 3x during the Connection Pack. Declarative directives in recipe JSON (recipes stay data, not code): `"$if"` conditions (`name` / `!name` / `name=value` / `name!=value`) drop phases, fields, or array elements; `{"$if", "$value"}` envelope for conditional scalar fields; transitions auto-rewire through dropped phases like unlinking a list node (chains of consecutive drops followed; a ref with nowhere to go is a compile error); `"$repeat"` inside `phases` expands one phase per array-param item with `${item.x}` / `${i}` / `${n}` / `${nextKey}` scope vars; `{"$map"}` builds derived arrays (quiz-show leaderboard summing every generated round); placeholders gained dotted paths (`${item.question}`, `${topic.title[0]}`). New `object` parameter type with `fields` (schema validation, coercion, synthetic defaults) + picker card widget (one labeled input per field; array sub-fields are comma-separated). Recipe upgrades that the old compiler couldn't express: One Voice `maxAttempts` (declared-but-blank optional params are falsy in conditions), Snowball `snowballAgain` (pairs → groups of 4 via a second conditional merge; share phase picked via drop-chain rewiring), Closer `partners` (new-each-tier rotatePairsFrom vs same-all-game reusePairsFrom). **Quiz Show shipped** — the recipe deferred since R3: teacher writes N questions (question/choices/correct cards in the picker), compiler emits N chained collect-choice rounds with optional Kahoot-style speed bonus + leaderboard; robot playthrough green (0 errors). All recipes verified compiling in both branches with 0 validation errors. Tests: `recipe-conditionals.test.js`. Test count: **685** (+16).
- 2026-06-10: **Two new phase types — buzz (24th) and estimate (25th)** — picked from a phase-ideas review as the highest classroom-energy-per-effort gaps. **buzz**: first-tap-wins buzzer rounds (trivia bee, spelling bee) — teacher asks questions out loud, first player to tap locks the buzzer (socket arrival order IS the buzz order — server-authoritative, pure referee `createBuzzState`/`applyBuzz`/`applyJudge`/`applyNextQuestion` in the handler), teacher judges ✓/✗ on the host (wrong = lockout for the question + reopen), "Next question" clears lockouts so one phase runs a whole informal round; outputs `scores` (scoreMap). Player UI is one giant red BUZZ button. **estimate**: numeric guessing — optional `answer` + `unit`, players resubmit freely until close, reveal shows the answer + class distribution + stats; scoring is rank-by-closeness so it is scale-free (`closest` winner-takes-all with tie-sharing, or `graduated` linear falloff — pure `engine/phases/estimate-scoring.js`); no answer = poll-the-room mode (stats only). Both: schema entries (new `number` field type), full editor support (catalog, sidebar forms, primary fields), Simple-view sentences, juice cues, simulator coverage (universal sim + robot playtest), connection-family forbidden (they award points). New game **Lightning Round** (buzz → estimate ×2 → leaderboard summing all three). Verified: robot playthrough green (0 errors, cross-phase score summing correct), live screenshot of host judging + both player states. Phase-ideas shortlist (branching votes, match, sort, secret-role, appreciation) added to NEXT-STEPS. Test count: **703** (+18).
- 2026-06-10: **Branching votes — vote.nextByWinner routes the game by outcome** — the biggest structural gap from the phase-ideas review: `next` can now depend on what the class decided. A pick-one vote on a LITERAL option list maps each option's text to a destination phase (`"nextByWinner": {"Enter the cave": "cave"}`); the winner routes the game, unmapped winners fall back to `next`, tie-breaks are deterministic. Wired everywhere a transition lives: state-machine legal-transition graph (first live run crashed without it — regression-tested), validator (target existence, key⊆options, ≥2 options, ref-candidates warning), unreachable-BFS + cycle detection (branches must go forward), recipe-compiler drop-rewiring, editor delete-relink + client validation. Vote phases also gained **teacher-typed literal options** (the "own options" odds-and-ends item) with rank-style list editing plus per-option "If this wins →" phase dropdowns; Simple view shows branches as sentences ("''Climb the spiral staircase'' wins → step 5"). AI generator taught the CYOA pattern. **Two latent bugs found and fixed**: (1) a late "Close Voting" click after the all-votes-in auto-advance ran the tally against the NEXT phase''s state and crashed the whole server — a real-classroom hazard (now kind-guarded + idempotent); (2) the universal simulator''s vote support never worked (listened for "vote-started"/emitted "cast-vote" — real events are "vote-start"/"submit-vote"), which explains old mid-game stalls in vote games. New game **Story Quest: The Locked Library** — class-steered CYOA, 2 branching votes, converging finale; robot playthrough green end-to-end. Test count: **712** (+9).
- 2026-06-10: **Hardening sweep (from the critical self-review)** — three fixes. (1) **Crash-race audit**: the close-voting crash class existed in rank/rate/wager too (closeRanking would iterate the NEXT phase''s state after an all-ranked auto-close raced a late host click). Every stateful phase now stamps `kind` on `room.phaseState`; all close/submit/resolve socket handlers kind-guard; closers + resolveWager are idempotent (`closed`/`resolved` flags). Plus a **global socket error trap** (every handler wrapped — one throwing room can''t kill every classroom on the server) and `process.on('unhandledRejection')` logs instead of crashing. (2) **Filter gaps**: relay text was broadcast unfiltered — `relay-submit` now passes the blocklist + 280-char cap (merge drafts were already filtered, contrary to the review''s claim — but their rejection notice yanked players to the wrong screen). `response-rejected` on the player is now section-aware: merge rejections show in the draft status, relay rejections reopen the turn to revise, collect behaves as before. (3) **CI**: `.github/workflows/test.yml` runs the suite on every push/PR — previously a push deployed to Render with zero automated gate. Verified: 712 tests green, dream-vacation robot playthrough (rank close + relay filter paths) clean.
- 2026-06-10: **Room snapshots — games survive server restarts (pre-August plan, item A)** — the existential gap: all room state lived in memory, so a Render redeploy/sleep/crash at minute 20 of a period silently killed every running game. Now every phase transition snapshots the room to Neon (`engine/room-snapshot.js`, `room_snapshots` table: engine position, phaseData, players + tokens, eliminations, kicked tokens, teacherPin, debounced 300ms, TTL-swept at 6h). A restarted server lazily resurrects the room when anyone rejoins: players via the existing token rebind; the host via a new `hostToken` (sessionStorage → `host-rejoin` on connect). Semantic: resume at the START of the interrupted phase (that phase''s mid-progress is lost; the game is not). Mid-foreach restores re-enter the foreach parent fresh. **Bonus fix**: host disconnect used to DELETE the room instantly — a teacher''s F5 or flaky projector laptop killed the game for the whole class; now the room holds 5 minutes for rejoin, and a host-rejoin re-enters the current phase. Wrong hostToken rejected; `?game=`/prototype launches never auto-rebind; `_sim-tmp-` rooms never persist. CI now supports **deploy-on-green** (RENDER_DEPLOY_HOOK secret + Render auto-deploy off — manual setup steps in the workflow comment). Verified end-to-end by `scripts/simulate-restart.js`: plays to mid-collect, SIGKILLs the server, starts a fresh one, host+player rejoin — roster, phase, and PIN all survive (9/9 checks). Test count: **716** (+4).
- 2026-06-10: **Chaos simulator (pre-August plan, item B) — and the five real bugs it found** — `scripts/simulate-chaos.js` + chaos mode in `services/simulator.js`: while the robot reactor plays a game, players randomly drop and reconnect mid-phase (token rebind), ghosts join with dead tokens, and stale/malformed/duplicate events spray the server; the game must still complete with every reconnect recognized. Suite covers all interactive phase types with zero AI spend (lightning-round, story-quest, snowball, one-voice, dream-vacation, charades-bowl). Fixed what it caught: (1) **player-id migration on reconnect** (`engine/id-migration.js`) — phases store player ids that went stale on rebind: turn describers got dead buttons after a wifi blip, relay players lost their turn, votes were silently dropped, and completed-phase score maps orphaned (reconnected kids vanished from leaderboards — a long-standing weakness now gone; deep in-place rewrite through phaseState/phaseData/foreachState); (2) **generic advance-phase now closes the current phase first** — the teacher console''s "Next step" used to blow past closeOneVoice/tally/closeRanking etc., losing outputs and leaving raw `{{tokens}}` on the next screen; (3) **close-submissions phase guard** — a stray/late close stored empty responses under whatever phase was current; (4) estimate reconnect-after-close shows results, and ESTIMATE_RESULTS/buzz broadcasts carry `phaseInstanceId`; (5) **empty rank/vote phases skip** instead of stranding students on an empty ballot. Sim-side fixes: rank-start host-payload false positive; charades "timeout" was a duplicate-phrase once-key in the reactor (game finishes in ~25s under full chaos, no special-casing needed). Final run: 6/6 games complete under maximum hostility — 59 reconnects (0 unrecognized), 18 ghosts, dozens of hostile payloads. Test count: **720** (+4 id-migration).
